-- ============================================================
-- ROYALE HIGH DEFINITIVE WARDROBE & CLOSET VIEWER v55
--
-- NEW FIXES & FEATURES IN v28:
-- 1. PRECISION CLOTHING SCANNER (ZERO HAIR CLONING):
--    - Registers default Roblox avatar parts at startup so your hair is 100% excluded.
--    - Scans all welded meshes, folders, and models across character limbs & EquippedStorage.
--    - Targets and clones strictly Royale High items: Heels, Skirts, Bodices, Sleeves, Wings, Halos!
-- 2. LAYER BAKER & SNAPSHOT CLONER:
--    - Bakes & locks the exact 3D meshes, textures, colors, and attachments into permanent client layers.
--    - Welds models directly to avatar limbs with Massless = true and CanCollide = false.
--    - Server try-on cleanup CANNOT delete client-protected baked layers.
--    - Dedicated "BAKE / LOCK" button + automatic baking on "WEAR / STACK".
-- 3. UNIVERSAL COLORING & IMAGE SORTING:
--    - Full color palette support for classic and modern items.
--    - Items with valid images always appear first in the scroll list.
-- 4. CHARACTER RESPAWN & LEG TRANSPARENCY:
--    - Restores stacked layers on CharacterAdded.
--    - Automatically manages leg transparency for heels and restores on Clear All.
-- ============================================================

if previewItemRemote then
    pcall(function()
        previewItemRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(_, targetPlayer, rawCat, actualAsset)
            if targetPlayer == lp or targetPlayer == nil then
                serverPreviewActive = true
                serverPreviewTick = tick()
                serverPreviewAsset = tostring(actualAsset or "")
            end
        end)
    end)

    pcall(function()
        exitPreviewRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
            serverPreviewActive = false

        end)

-- Zero-overhead Anti-Anchor guard (watches only HumanoidRootPart instead of hooking global __newindex)
local function bindHrpAntiAnchor(char)
    if not char then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
        if hrp then
            hrp:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Anchored"):Connect(function()
                if hrp.Anchored then hrp.Anchored = false end
            end)
            if hrp.Anchored then hrp.Anchored = false end
        end
        local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
        if hum then
            if RHRigColor.snapshotOrigHipHeight then
                RHRigColor.snapshotOrigHipHeight(char)
            end
            hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("HipHeight"):Connect(function()
                if not RHRigColor.applyingHipHeight and RHRigColor.hasActiveTargetHipHeight and RHRigColor.hasActiveTargetHipHeight() ~= nil then
                    task.defer(function()
                        if lp.Character == char and RHRigColor.syncHipHeight then
                            RHRigColor.syncHipHeight(char)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end
lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(bindHrpAntiAnchor)

local function getGuiParent()
    if gethui then return gethui() end
    local ok, cg = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    if ok and cg then return cg end
    return pg
end

-- Cleanup older versions
for _, name in ipairs({
    "RH_Wardrobe_v16","RH_Wardrobe_v17","RH_Wardrobe_v18","RH_Wardrobe_v19",
    "RH_Wardrobe_v20","RH_Wardrobe_v21","RH_Wardrobe_v22","RH_Wardrobe_v23",
    "RH_Wardrobe_v24","RH_Wardrobe_v51","RH_Wardrobe_v52","RH_Wardrobe_v53",
    "RH_Wardrobe_v54","RH_Wardrobe_v55","RH_Wardrobe_Definitive"
}) do
    pcall(function()
        local old = getGuiParent():FindFirstChild(name)
        if old then old:Destroy() end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local old2 = pg:FindFirstChild(name)
        if old2 then old2:Destroy() end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- 1. SELECTIVE FREEZE NEUTRALIZER & OVERLAY SUPPRESSOR
-- ============================================================
local function disableFreezeOnlyConnections()
    if getconnections and previewItemRemote then
        pcall(function()
            for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(previewItemRemote.OnClientEvent)) do
                pcall(function()
                    local fn = conn.Function
                    local isFreeze = false
                    if getconstants and fn then
                        for _, c in ipairs(getconstants(fn)) do
                            if c == "ExitTryOnNonWings" or c == "PrimaryPart" or c == "Anchored" then
                                isFreeze = true
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if isFreeze then
                        conn:Disable()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function suppressGuiObj(desc)
    if not desc then return end
    if desc:IsA("BillboardGui") or desc:IsA("SurfaceGui") or desc:IsA("ScreenGui") then
        desc.Enabled = false
        if not desc:GetAttribute("RH_Suppressed") then
            desc:SetAttribute("RH_Suppressed", true)
            pcall(function()
                desc:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
                    if desc.Enabled then desc.Enabled = false end
                end)
            end)
        end
    elseif desc:IsA("GuiObject") then
        desc.Visible = false
        if not desc:GetAttribute("RH_Suppressed") then
            desc:SetAttribute("RH_Suppressed", true)
            pcall(function()
                desc:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
                    if desc.Visible then desc.Visible = false end
                end)
            end)
        end
    end
end

local function isOverheadTryOnBillboard(inst)
    if not inst then return false end
    local bb = (inst:IsA("BillboardGui") or inst:IsA("SurfaceGui")) and inst or inst:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BillboardGui") or inst:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("SurfaceGui")
    if not bb then return false end
    local nl = inst.Name:lower()
    if nl:find("tryon") or nl:find("try_on") or nl:find("try on") then return true end
    if (inst:IsA("TextLabel") or inst:IsA("TextButton")) then
        local tl = (inst.Text or ""):lower()
        if tl:find("try on") or tl:find("trying on") or tl:find("tryon") then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local function hideTryOnOverlays()
    pcall(function()
        -- Only hide ExitTryOn buttons in HUD/Shop (never touch Shop's "Try On" buttons!)
        local hud = pg:FindFirstChild("HUD")
        if hud then
            for _, ch in ipairs(hud:GetDescendants()) do
                if ch:IsA("GuiObject") then
                    local nl = ch.Name:lower()
                    local isTryBanner = nl:find("exittryon") or nl:find("tryonmode")
                    if not isTryBanner and (ch:IsA("TextLabel") or ch:IsA("TextButton")) then
                        local tl = (ch.Text or ""):lower()
                        if tl:find("try on mode") or tl:find("exit try on") then
                            isTryBanner = true
                        end
                    end
                    if isTryBanner then
                        suppressGuiObj(ch)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local shop = pg:FindFirstChild("Shop")
        if shop then
            local ex = shop:FindFirstChild("ExitTryOnNonWings", true)
            if ex and ex:IsA("GuiObject") then suppressGuiObj(ex) end
        end
        -- Hide overhead TryOn BillboardGuis on Character (Head/Torso)
        local char = lp.Character
        if char then
            for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if isOverheadTryOnBillboard(d) then
                    local bb = (d:IsA("BillboardGui") or d:IsA("SurfaceGui")) and d or d:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BillboardGui") or d:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("SurfaceGui")
                    if bb then suppressGuiObj(bb) end
                end
            end
        end
        for _, gui in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
            if gui.Name ~= "RH_Wardrobe_v55" and gui.Name ~= "Shop" then
                for _, bb in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                    if bb:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                        for _, d in ipairs(bb:GetDescendants()) do
                            if isOverheadTryOnBillboard(d) then suppressGuiObj(bb) break end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

disableFreezeOnlyConnections()
hideTryOnOverlays()

-- Lightweight listeners (only HUD ExitTryOn & Character Head/root BillboardGuis, zero full-PlayerGui lag!)
task.spawn(function()
    local hud = pg:WaitForChild("HUD", 10)
    if hud then
        hud.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.Name:find("ExitTryOn") then
                suppressGuiObj(ch)
            end
        end)
    end
end)

local function hookCharOverheadTryOn(char)
    if not char then return end
    local function checkPart(p)
        if p:IsA("BillboardGui") or p:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
            task.defer(function()
                if isOverheadTryOnBillboard(p) then suppressGuiObj(p) return end
                for _, d in ipairs(p:GetDescendants()) do
                    if isOverheadTryOnBillboard(d) then suppressGuiObj(p) break end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    char.ChildAdded:Connect(checkPart)
    for _, limbName in ipairs({"Head", "UpperTorso", "Torso", "HumanoidRootPart"}) do
        local limb = char:FindFirstChild(limbName)
        if limb then
            limb.ChildAdded:Connect(checkPart)
            for _, ch in ipairs(limb:GetChildren()) do checkPart(ch) end
        end
    end
end
if lp.Character then pcall(hookCharOverheadTryOn, lp.Character) end
lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(hookCharOverheadTryOn)

    ["HumanoidRootPart"]=true,["Head"]=true,["Torso"]=true,
    ["UpperTorso"]=true,["LowerTorso"]=true,["Left Arm"]=true,
    ["Right Arm"]=true,["Left Leg"]=true,["Right Leg"]=true,

    ["LeftUpperLeg"]=true,["LeftLowerLeg"]=true,["LeftFoot"]=true,
    ["RightUpperLeg"]=true,["RightLowerLeg"]=true,["RightFoot"]=true
}

local function releasePlayerMovement()
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return end

    -- 1. Unanchor all character parts and primary part
    local pp = char.PrimaryPart or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if pp and pp.Anchored then pp.Anchored = false end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp and hrp.Anchored then hrp.Anchored = false end

    for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Anchored then
            part.Anchored = false
        end
    end

    -- 2. Humanoid walkspeed, jumping, sitting, platformstand
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        if hum.PlatformStand then hum.PlatformStand = false end
        if hum.Sit then hum.Sit = false end
        if hum.WalkSpeed < 16 then hum.WalkSpeed = 16 end
        if hum.JumpPower and hum.JumpPower < 50 then hum.JumpPower = 50 end
        if hum.JumpHeight and hum.JumpHeight < 7.2 then hum.JumpHeight = 7.2 end

        -- Stop only forced freeze animations (preserve item idle/hold/pose animations!)
        for _, track in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            local tn = track.Animation and track.Animation.Name:lower() or ""
            if tn:find("freeze") then
                pcall(function() track:Stop(0.05) end)
            end
        end
    end

    -- 3. Unbind all 10 known ContextActionService freeze actions in Royale High
    local freezeActions = {
        "ControlsDisabled", "DisableControlsCutscene", "IntroScriptDisableControls",
        "RH3CutsceneControlsDisabled", "FreezeMovement", "ShopFreeze", "TryOnFreeze",
        "DisableMovement", "CharacterCreationFreeze", "TryOnControl"
    }
    for _, act in ipairs(freezeActions) do
        pcall(function() ContextActionService:UnbindAction(act) end)
    end

    -- 4. Enable Roblox PlayerModule controls
    pcall(function()
        local pm = lp.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
        if pm then
            local mod = require(pm)
            if mod and mod.GetControls then
                local ctrl = mod:GetControls()
                if ctrl and ctrl.Enable then
                    ctrl:Enable()
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- 5. Only trigger ExitPreview.OnClientEvent connections that handle anchoring (NEVER close PreviewTogglesFrame or ColorsGui!)
    if exitPreviewRemote and getconnections then
        pcall(function()
            for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(exitPreviewRemote.OnClientEvent)) do
                local fn = conn.Function
                local isAnchorReset = false
                if getconstants and fn then
                    for _, c in ipairs(getconstants(fn)) do
                        if c == "PrimaryPart" or c == "Anchored" or c == "ExitTryOnNonWings" then
                            isAnchorReset = true
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
                if isAnchorReset then
                    pcall(function() conn:Fire() end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- 6. Hide all TryOn billboard HUD elements
    pcall(hideTryOnOverlays)
end

local enforceHiddenBodyParts = nil
local function unanchorCharacter()
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return end

    local pp = char.PrimaryPart or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if pp and pp.Anchored then pp.Anchored = false end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp and hrp.Anchored then hrp.Anchored = false end

    for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Anchored then
            part.Anchored = false
        end
    end

    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        if hum.PlatformStand then hum.PlatformStand = false end
        if hum.Sit then hum.Sit = false end
        if hum.WalkSpeed < 16 then hum.WalkSpeed = 16 end
    end
    if enforceHiddenBodyParts then
        pcall(enforceHiddenBodyParts)
    end
end

local activeCustomFlyTrack = nil
local activeCustomFlyAnimId = nil

local function stopCustomFlyTrack()
    if activeCustomFlyTrack then
        pcall(function() activeCustomFlyTrack:Stop(0.15) end)
        activeCustomFlyTrack = nil
        activeCustomFlyAnimId = nil
    end
end

local function updateCustomFlyAnimations()
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then stopCustomFlyTrack() return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local bakedFolder = getBakedFolder and getBakedFolder(false) or char:FindFirstChild("RH_BakedWardrobe")
    if not hum or not hrp or not bakedFolder then
        stopCustomFlyTrack()
        return
    end

    -- Detect Royale High flight state (PlatformStand / Physics state / flight BodyMovers)
    local isFlying = hum.PlatformStand
                  or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                  or hrp:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BodyVelocity") ~= nil
                  or hrp:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("LinearVelocity") ~= nil
                  or hrp:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BodyPosition") ~= nil
                  or hrp:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("AlignPosition") ~= nil

    if not isFlying then
        stopCustomFlyTrack()
        return
    end

    local targetAnim = nil
    for _, baked in ipairs(bakedFolder:GetChildren()) do
        local cf = baked:FindFirstChild("CustomFly", true)
        if cf then
            local animFolder = cf:FindFirstChild("Animations") or cf
            for _, a in ipairs(animFolder:GetChildren()) do
                if a:IsA("Animation") and a.AnimationId ~= "" then
                    targetAnim = a
                    break
                end
            end
            if targetAnim then break end
        end
    end

    if not targetAnim then
        stopCustomFlyTrack()
        return
    end

    if activeCustomFlyAnimId ~= targetAnim.AnimationId or (activeCustomFlyTrack and not activeCustomFlyTrack.IsPlaying) then
        stopCustomFlyTrack()
        local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or hum
        local ok, tr = pcall(function() return animator:LoadAnimation(targetAnim) end)
        if ok and tr then
            tr.Looped = true
            tr.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
            tr:Play(0.15)
            activeCustomFlyTrack = tr
            activeCustomFlyAnimId = targetAnim.AnimationId
        end
    end
end

local lastUnanchorTick = 0
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    local now = tick()
    if now - lastUnanchorTick < 0.12 then return end
    lastUnanchorTick = now
    unanchorCharacter()
    pcall(updateCustomFlyAnimations)
end)

-- Instant unfreeze when tapping screen or moving joystick
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
       or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.W or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A
       or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.S or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D
       or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space then
        releasePlayerMovement()
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(1.5) do
        disableFreezeOnlyConnections()
        pcall(function()
            ContextActionService:UnbindAction("FreezeMovement")
            ContextActionService:UnbindAction("ShopFreeze")
            ContextActionService:UnbindAction("TryOnFreeze")
            ContextActionService:UnbindAction("DisableMovement")
            ContextActionService:UnbindAction("CharacterCreationFreeze")
            ContextActionService:UnbindAction("TryOnControl")
            ContextActionService:UnbindAction("ControlsDisabled")
            ContextActionService:UnbindAction("RH3CutsceneControlsDisabled")
        end)
    end
end)

local function getBakedClosetName()
    return "RH_BakedCloset_" .. tostring(lp.UserId)
end

function getBakedFolder(createIfMissing)
    -- Store clones directly inside workspace (100% immune to Royale High Character resets / ClearAllChildren!)
    local cname = getBakedClosetName()
    local bf = workspace:FindFirstChild(cname)
    if not bf and createIfMissing then
        bf = Instance.new("Folder")
        bf.Name = cname
        bf:SetAttribute("RH_ClientCloset", true)
        bf.Parent = workspace
    end
    return bf
end

function getPreviewFolder(createIfMissing)
    local pname = "RH_PreviewStage_" .. tostring(lp.UserId)
    local pf = workspace:FindFirstChild(pname)
    if not pf and createIfMissing then
        pf = Instance.new("Folder")
        pf.Name = pname
        pf:SetAttribute("RH_PreviewStage", true)
        pf.Parent = workspace
    end
    return pf
end

function clearPreviewStage()
    if activeHaloBobConns then
        for k, conn in pairs(activeHaloBobConns) do
            if type(k) == "string" and k:sub(1, 8) == "Preview_" then
                pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
                activeHaloBobConns[k] = nil
            end
        end
    end
    local pf = getPreviewFolder(false)
    if pf then pcall(function() pf:Destroy() end) end
    local removedMap = nil
    for k, partsMap in pairs(hiddenBodyPartsBySlot) do
        if type(k) == "string" and k:sub(1, 8) == "Preview_" then
            removedMap = removedMap or {}
            for pn, v in pairs(partsMap) do
                if v then removedMap[pn] = true end
            end
            hiddenBodyPartsBySlot[k] = nil
        end
    end
    if removedMap and restoreUnhiddenBodyParts then
        pcall(restoreUnhiddenBodyParts, removedMap)
    end
    activePreviewItem = nil
    previewBeforeSet = {}
    if RHRigColor.syncHipHeight then
        pcall(function() RHRigColor.syncHipHeight(lp.Character) end)
    end
end

-- Migrate any legacy RH_BakedWardrobe out of Character into safe workspace closet
pcall(function()
    local char = lp.Character
    local oldBf = char and char:FindFirstChild("RH_BakedWardrobe")
    if oldBf then
        local safeBf = getBakedFolder(true)
        for _, ch in ipairs(oldBf:GetChildren()) do
            ch.Parent = safeBf
        end
        oldBf:Destroy()
    end
end)

local legPartNames = {
    "LeftLeg", "RightLeg", "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot",
    "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"
}

function RHRigColor.getSkinColor(char)
    if not char then return Color3.fromRGB(255, 224, 189) end
    local hd = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if hd and hd:IsA("BasePart") then return hd.Color end
    local bc = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
    if bc then return bc.HeadColor3 or bc.TorsoColor3 end
    for _, nm in ipairs({"UpperTorso", "Torso", "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm"}) do
        local bp = char:FindFirstChild(nm)
        if bp and bp:IsA("BasePart") and bp.Transparency < 0.99 then return bp.Color end
    end
    return Color3.fromRGB(255, 224, 189)
end

function RHRigColor.hasActiveTargetHipHeight()
    local best = nil
    local pf = getPreviewFolder(false)
    if pf then
        for _, m in ipairs(pf:GetChildren()) do
            local hh = m:GetAttribute("RH_TargetHipHeight")
            if type(hh) == "number" and (not best or hh > best) then best = hh end
        end
    end
    local bf = getBakedFolder(false)
    if bf then
        for _, m in ipairs(bf:GetChildren()) do
            local hh = m:GetAttribute("RH_TargetHipHeight")
            if type(hh) == "number" and (not best or hh > best) then best = hh end
        end
    end
    return best
end

function RHRigColor.snapshotOrigHipHeight(char)
    if RHRigColor.origHipHeight ~= nil or not char then return end
    if RHRigColor.hasActiveTargetHipHeight() ~= nil or RHRigColor.appliedHipHeight ~= nil then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        RHRigColor.origHipHeight = hum.HipHeight
    end
end

function RHRigColor.syncHipHeight(char)
    if not char or RHRigColor.applyingHipHeight then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return end

    -- Never overwrite live server Try-On preview of a new pair of Heels while previewing!
    if serverPreviewActive and activePreviewItem and activePreviewItem.cat == "Heels" then
        local pf = getPreviewFolder(false)
        local hasPreviewHeelHH = false
        if pf then
            for _, ch in ipairs(pf:GetChildren()) do
                if type(ch:GetAttribute("RH_TargetHipHeight")) == "number" then
                    hasPreviewHeelHH = true
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if not hasPreviewHeelHH then return end
    end

    local targetHH = nil
    local pf = getPreviewFolder(false)
    if pf then
        for _, ch in ipairs(pf:GetChildren()) do
            local hh = ch:GetAttribute("RH_TargetHipHeight")
            if type(hh) == "number" then
                if not targetHH or hh > targetHH then targetHH = hh end
            end
        end
    end
    if not targetHH then
        local bf = getBakedFolder(false)
        if bf then
            for _, ch in ipairs(bf:GetChildren()) do
                local hh = ch:GetAttribute("RH_TargetHipHeight")
                if type(hh) == "number" then
                    if not targetHH or hh > targetHH then targetHH = hh end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if targetHH then
        if RHRigColor.origHipHeight == nil then
            RHRigColor.origHipHeight = hum.HipHeight
        end
        if math.abs(hum.HipHeight - targetHH) > 0.02 then
            RHRigColor.applyingHipHeight = true
            RHRigColor.appliedHipHeight = targetHH
            pcall(function() hum.HipHeight = targetHH end)
            RHRigColor.applyingHipHeight = false
        else
            RHRigColor.appliedHipHeight = targetHH
        end
    elseif RHRigColor.appliedHipHeight ~= nil and RHRigColor.origHipHeight ~= nil then
        if math.abs(hum.HipHeight - RHRigColor.origHipHeight) > 0.02 then
            RHRigColor.applyingHipHeight = true
            pcall(function() hum.HipHeight = RHRigColor.origHipHeight end)
            RHRigColor.applyingHipHeight = false
        end
        RHRigColor.appliedHipHeight = nil
    end
end

function RHRigColor.applySkinToModel(model, char)
    if not model or not char then return end
    local skinColor = RHRigColor.getSkinColor(char)
    local hasColoredSkin = false
    local function handleDesc(d)
        if d:IsA("BasePart") and (d.Name:lower() == "coloredleg" or d:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") == true) then
            hasColoredSkin = true
            d:SetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart", true)
            d.Color = skinColor
            for _, ch in ipairs(d:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
                    ch:SetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart", true)
                    pcall(function() ch.Color = skinColor end)
                elseif ch:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
                    ch:SetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart", true)
                    pcall(function() ch.VertexColor = Vector3.new(skinColor.R, skinColor.G, skinColor.B) end)
                end
            end
        elseif d:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") and (d.Name:lower() == "coloredleg" or (d.Parent and d.Parent.Name:lower() == "coloredleg")) then
            hasColoredSkin = true
            d:SetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart", true)
            pcall(function() d.Color = skinColor end)
        end
    end
    if model:IsA("BasePart") then handleDesc(model) end
    for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do handleDesc(d) end
    if hasColoredSkin and model.SetAttribute then
        pcall(function() model:SetAttribute("RH_HasColoredSkin", true) end)
    end
end

function RHRigColor.isSkinPart(p)
    if not p then return true end
    if p.GetAttribute and p:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") == true then return true end
    local pn = tostring(p.Name or ""):lower()
    if standardBodyParts[p.Name] or p.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" or p.Name == "Head" then
        return true
    end
    if pn == "coloredleg" or pn:find("skintone")
       or pn == "leg" or pn == "arm" or pn == "hand" or pn == "foot" or pn == "thigh" or pn == "body" then
        return true
    end
    if (pn:find("skin") and not pn:find("silk")) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function RHRigColor.parseGroupFromName(name)
    local s = tostring(name or "")
    local n = tonumber(s)
    if not n then
        n = tonumber(s:match("^.-%((%d+)%)%s*$"))
            or tonumber(s:match("^[Cc]olor%s*(%d+)$"))
            or tonumber(s:match("^[Mm]ulti[Cc]olor%s*(%d+)$"))
    end
    if n then
        n = math.floor(n)
        if n >= 1 and n <= 12 then return n end
    end
    return nil
end

function RHRigColor.isColorEffect(d)
    return d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("Trail") or d:IsA("Light")
end

function RHRigColor.isVisualColorChild(d)
    return d:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or d:IsA("SpecialMesh") or d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture")
end

function RHRigColor.formatModel(model, b64Key)
    if not model then return false end
    if model:GetAttribute("RH_ColorFormatted") and model:GetAttribute("RH_ColorFmtKey") == tostring(b64Key or "") then
        return model:GetAttribute("RH_HasNumberedGroups") == true
    end

    local BK = b64Key or model:GetAttribute("RH_CustomB64Key")
    local inConfig = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    for _, f in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
        if f.Name == "Configuration" or f.Name == "Colors" or f.Name == "InteractSettings" then
            inConfig[f] = true
            for _, sub in ipairs(f:GetDescendants()) do inConfig[sub] = true end
        end
    end

    local isHelperSet = (type(HELPER_TRANSPARENT_PARTS) == "table") and HELPER_TRANSPARENT_PARTS or {}
    local ownGroup = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    local foundAnyNumber = false
    local maxG = 0

    local function bumpMax(g)
        if g and g >= 1 and g <= 12 then
            foundAnyNumber = true
            if g > maxG then maxG = g end
        end
    end

    local function readExplicitGroup(inst)
        local mc = inst:GetAttribute("MultiColor")
        if mc ~= nil then
            local gn = tonumber(mc)
            if gn and gn >= 1 and gn <= 12 then return math.floor(gn) end
        end
        local mv = inst:FindFirstChild("MultiColor")
        if mv and (mv:IsA("IntValue") or mv:IsA("NumberValue")) then
            local gn = tonumber(mv.Value)
            if gn and gn >= 1 and gn <= 12 then return math.floor(gn) end
        end
        return RHRigColor.parseGroupFromName(inst.Name)
    end

    local function hasAncName(d, pat)
        local cur = d.Parent
        while cur and cur ~= model do
            if tostring(cur.Name or ""):lower():find(pat, 1, true) then return true end
            cur = cur.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    -- Pass 1: record explicit MultiColor / name numbers & per-RBXM channel overrides
    local descs = model:GetDescendants()
    if model:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(descs, model) end

    for _, d in ipairs(descs) do
        if not inConfig[d] then
            if d:IsA("Folder") or d:IsA("Model") then
                local g = readExplicitGroup(d)
                if g then ownGroup[d] = g; bumpMax(g) end
            elseif d:IsA("BasePart") then
                if d.Name:lower() == "coloredleg" or d:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") then
                    d:SetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart", true)
                elseif not isHelperSet[d.Name] and not RHRigColor.isSkinPart(d) then
                    local dl = d.Name:lower()
                    local g = readExplicitGroup(d)
                    if BK == "hair" then
                        g = (dl:find("sphere", 1, true) or hasAncName(d, "tie") or hasAncName(d, "band")) and 2 or 1
                    elseif BK == "glimmering" or BK == "alien" then
                        if dl == "neon" or dl:find("glow", 1, true) then g = 2
                        elseif dl == "ring" or dl:find("bread", 1, true) then g = 1 end
                    elseif BK == "val20" then
                        if dl:find("heart", 1, true) or dl == "ring" or dl:find("snowflake", 1, true) then g = 2
                        elseif dl == "meshpart" or dl == "non" then g = 1 end
                    elseif BK == "lucky21" and not g then
                        if dl:find("rainbow", 1, true) or dl:find("swirl", 1, true) or hasAncName(d, "rainbow") then
                            g = 3
                        end
                    elseif BK == "winter22" and not g then
                        if dl:find("crystal", 1, true) or dl:find("glow", 1, true) or hasAncName(d, "crystal") then
                            g = 3
                        end
                    end
                    if g then ownGroup[d] = g; bumpMax(g) end
                end
            elseif RHRigColor.isVisualColorChild(d) then
                local dl = d.Name:lower()
                if dl == "coloredleg" or (d.Parent and (d.Parent.Name:lower() == "coloredleg" or d.Parent:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart"))) then
                    d:SetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart", true)
                else
                    local g = readExplicitGroup(d)
                    if BK == "hair" then
                        local pl = d.Parent and d.Parent.Name:lower() or ""
                        g = (pl:find("sphere", 1, true) or dl:find("sphere", 1, true)) and 2 or 1
                    elseif BK == "glimmering" or BK == "alien" then
                        local pl = d.Parent and d.Parent.Name:lower() or ""
                        if pl == "neon" or dl == "neon" then g = 2
                        elseif pl == "ring" or pl:find("bread", 1, true) then g = 1 end
                    elseif BK == "val20" then
                        local pl = d.Parent and d.Parent.Name:lower() or ""
                        if pl:find("heart", 1, true) or pl == "ring" or pl:find("snowflake", 1, true) then g = 2
                        elseif pl == "meshpart" or pl == "non" then g = 1 end
                    elseif BK == "lucky21" and not g then
                        local pl = d.Parent and d.Parent.Name:lower() or ""
                        if pl:find("rainbow", 1, true) or pl:find("swirl", 1, true) or hasAncName(d, "rainbow") then g = 3 end
                    end
                    if g then ownGroup[d] = g; bumpMax(g) end
                end
            elseif RHRigColor.isColorEffect(d) then
                if not (d.Parent and d.Parent:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart")) then
                    local g = readExplicitGroup(d)
                    local dl = d.Name:lower()
                    if not g then
                        if BK == "glimmering" or BK == "alien" or BK == "val20" then
                            g = 2
                        elseif BK == "lucky21" then
                            g = 3
                        elseif BK == "winter22" then
                            if hasAncName(d, "crystal") or hasAncName(d, "sparkle") or hasAncName(d, "nocolor") or (d.Parent and d.Parent.Name == "NoColor") then
                                g = 3
                            end
                        elseif BK == "everfriend24" then
                            g = 6
                        elseif BK == "everfriend25" then
                            if dl:find("rainbow", 1, true) or hasAncName(d, "rainbow") then
                                g = 10
                            elseif d:IsA("Light") or dl:find("glow", 1, true) or hasAncName(d, "glow") then
                                g = 9
                            else
                                g = 10
                            end
                        elseif BK == "everfriend26" then
                            g = 6
                        elseif BK == "bodice" then
                            g = 3
                        elseif BK == "scepter" then
                            g = 6
                        end
                    end
                    if g then ownGroup[d] = g; bumpMax(g) end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Check Colors Configuration folder too (if model defines MultiColor1..N)
    local cfgColors = model:FindFirstChild("Colors", true)
    if cfgColors then
        for _, cVal in ipairs(cfgColors:GetChildren()) do
            local gn = tonumber(tostring(cVal.Name):match("%d+"))
            if gn and gn >= 1 and gn <= 12 then bumpMax(gn) end
        end
    end

    if BK and CUSTOM_RBXM_REGISTRY and CUSTOM_RBXM_REGISTRY[BK] then
        foundAnyNumber = true
        if (CUSTOM_RBXM_REGISTRY[BK].maxColors or 0) > maxG then
            maxG = CUSTOM_RBXM_REGISTRY[BK].maxColors
        end
    end

    -- Pass 2: Resolve & stamp MultiColor cleanly on BaseParts first (so SurfaceAppearance / FX can inherit!)
    local function findAncestorGroup(inst)
        local cur = inst.Parent
        while cur and cur ~= model do
            if ownGroup[cur] then return ownGroup[cur] end
            local pg = RHRigColor.parseGroupFromName(cur.Name)
            if pg then return pg end
            cur = cur.Parent
        end
        return nil
    end

    for _, d in ipairs(descs) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") and not inConfig[d] and not isHelperSet[d.Name] and not RHRigColor.isSkinPart(d) then
            local g = ownGroup[d] or findAncestorGroup(d)
            if not g then
                for _, ch in ipairs(d:GetChildren()) do
                    if ch:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") and ownGroup[ch] then
                        g = ownGroup[ch]
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            if not g and foundAnyNumber then
                g = 1
            end
            if g then
                ownGroup[d] = g
                bumpMax(g)
                d:SetAttribute("MultiColor", g)
            end
        end
    end

    -- Pass 3: Resolve SurfaceAppearance / SpecialMesh / Decal / Texture & ParticleEmitters / Lights / Beams / Trails
    for _, d in ipairs(descs) do
        if not inConfig[d] and not d:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") then
            if RHRigColor.isVisualColorChild(d) then
                local par = d.Parent
                local parIsSkin = par and (par:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") == true or (par:IsA("BasePart") and RHRigColor.isSkinPart(par) and not isHelperSet[par.Name]))
                if not parIsSkin then
                    local g = ownGroup[d] or (par and ownGroup[par]) or findAncestorGroup(d)
                    if not g and foundAnyNumber then g = 1 end
                    if g then
                        ownGroup[d] = g
                        bumpMax(g)
                        d:SetAttribute("MultiColor", g)
                    end
                end
            elseif RHRigColor.isColorEffect(d) then
                local par = d.Parent
                local parIsSkin = par and par:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") == true
                if not parIsSkin then
                    local g = ownGroup[d] or (par and ownGroup[par]) or findAncestorGroup(d)
                    if not g and foundAnyNumber then
                        g = (maxG >= 2) and 2 or 1
                    end
                    if g then
                        ownGroup[d] = g
                        bumpMax(g)
                        d:SetAttribute("MultiColor", g)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    model:SetAttribute("RH_ColorFormatted", true)
    model:SetAttribute("RH_ColorFmtKey", tostring(BK or ""))
    model:SetAttribute("RH_HasNumberedGroups", foundAnyNumber)
    if maxG > 0 then
        model:SetAttribute("RH_MaxColorGroup", maxG)
    end
    return foundAnyNumber
end

function RHRigColor.countModelGroups(m)
    if not m then return 0 end
    RHRigColor.formatModel(m, m:GetAttribute("RH_CustomB64Key"))
    local maxG = tonumber(m:GetAttribute("RH_MaxColorGroup")) or 0
    for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
        if not d:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") then
            local mc = tonumber(d:GetAttribute("MultiColor"))
            if mc and mc > maxG and mc <= 12 then maxG = mc end
        end
    end
    local cfgColors = m:FindFirstChild("Colors", true)
    if cfgColors then
        for _, cVal in ipairs(cfgColors:GetChildren()) do
            local gn = tonumber(tostring(cVal.Name):match("%d+"))
            if gn and gn > maxG and gn <= 12 then maxG = gn end
        end
    end
    return maxG
end

function RHRigColor.getAssetColorMeta(assetName)
    local key = tostring(assetName or "")
    if key == "" then return nil end
    if RHRigColor.detailedAssetCache[key] then
        return RHRigColor.detailedAssetCache[key]
    end
    local info = { numGroups = nil, colorNames = nil }
    pcall(function()
        local inv = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Inventory")
        local gn = inv and inv:FindFirstChild("GetNumColorGroups")
        if gn and gn:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
            local res = gn:InvokeServer(key)
            if type(res) == "number" and res >= 1 and res <= 12 then
                info.numGroups = res
            end
        end
        local gd = inv and inv:FindFirstChild("GetDetailedAsset")
        if gd and gd:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
            local det = gd:InvokeServer(key, nil, true)
            if type(det) == "table" then
                if type(det.NumColorGroups) == "number" and det.NumColorGroups >= 1 and det.NumColorGroups <= 12 then
                    info.numGroups = math.max(info.numGroups or 0, det.NumColorGroups)
                end
                if type(det.ColorNames) == "table" and #det.ColorNames > 0 then
                    info.colorNames = det.ColorNames
                    info.numGroups = math.max(info.numGroups or 0, #det.ColorNames)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    RHRigColor.detailedAssetCache[key] = info
    return info
end

function RHRigColor.applyColorToModel(m, char, c3, group, texOverride)
    if not m or not c3 then return false end
    group = tonumber(group) or 1
    local hasNumbers = RHRigColor.formatModel(m, m:GetAttribute("RH_CustomB64Key"))
    local maxGroups = tonumber(m:GetAttribute("RH_MaxColorGroup")) or (hasNumbers and 2 or 2)
    local isHelperSet = (type(HELPER_TRANSPARENT_PARTS) == "table") and HELPER_TRANSPARENT_PARTS or {}

    local function matchesGroup(p, isUnnumberedTarget)
        local mc = p:GetAttribute("MultiColor")
        if mc ~= nil then
            return tonumber(mc) == group
        end
        if hasNumbers then
            local pn = RHRigColor.parseGroupFromName(p.Name)
            if pn == group then return true end
            local par = p.Parent
            while par and par ~= char and par ~= m do
                local pmc = par:GetAttribute("MultiColor")
                if pmc ~= nil then return tonumber(pmc) == group end
                local pg = RHRigColor.parseGroupFromName(par.Name)
                if pg then return pg == group end
                par = par.Parent
            end
            return false
        end
        if maxGroups <= 1 and group == 1 then
            return true
        end
        if RHRigColor.isColorEffect(p) then
            return group == 2
        end
        local n = (p.Name .. " " .. (p.Parent and p.Parent.Name or "")):lower()
        local isGroup2 = n:find("2") or n:find("trim") or n:find("lace") or n:find("accent")
                      or n:find("ribbon") or n:find("bow") or n:find("secondary") or n:find("buckle")
                      or n:find("detail") or n:find("gem") or n:find("jewel") or n:find("metal")
                      or n:find("glow") or n:find("sparkle")
        if group == 2 then
            return isGroup2
        elseif group == 1 then
            return not isGroup2 or n:find("1") or n:find("main") or n:find("base")
        end
        return false
    end

    local function colorSinglePart(p)
        if p:IsA("MeshPart") then
            if texOverride then
                pcall(function() p.TextureID = texOverride end)
            else
                pcall(function() p.TextureID = "" end)
            end
        end
        for _, ch in ipairs(p:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("SpecialMesh") and not ch:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") then
                local chMc = ch:GetAttribute("MultiColor")
                if not hasNumbers or chMc == nil or tonumber(chMc) == group then
                    pcall(function()
                        ch.TextureId = texOverride or ""
                        ch.VertexColor = Vector3.new(c3.R, c3.G, c3.B)
                    end)
                end
            elseif ch:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") and not ch:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") then
                local saGroup = ch:GetAttribute("MultiColor") or RHRigColor.parseGroupFromName(ch.Name)
                if not hasNumbers or saGroup == nil or tonumber(saGroup) == group then
                    pcall(function() ch.Color = c3 end)
                    if texOverride then pcall(function() ch.ColorMap = texOverride end) end
                end
            end
        end
        p.Color = c3
    end

    local allTargets = m:GetDescendants()
    if m:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(allTargets, m) end

    local coloredCount = 0
    for _, d in ipairs(allTargets) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") and not isHelperSet[d.Name] and not RHRigColor.isSkinPart(d) then
            if matchesGroup(d) then
                colorSinglePart(d)
                coloredCount = coloredCount + 1
            end
        elseif d:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") and not d:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") then
            local par = d.Parent
            local parIsSkin = par and (par:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") == true or (par:IsA("BasePart") and RHRigColor.isSkinPart(par) and not isHelperSet[par.Name]))
            if not parIsSkin and matchesGroup(d) then
                pcall(function() d.Color = c3 end)
                if texOverride then pcall(function() d.ColorMap = texOverride end) end
                coloredCount = coloredCount + 1
            end
        elseif d:IsA("SpecialMesh") and not d:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") then
            local par = d.Parent
            local parIsSkin = par and (par:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") == true or (par:IsA("BasePart") and RHRigColor.isSkinPart(par) and not isHelperSet[par.Name]))
            if not parIsSkin and (matchesGroup(d) or (par and matchesGroup(par))) then
                pcall(function()
                    d.TextureId = texOverride or ""
                    d.VertexColor = Vector3.new(c3.R, c3.G, c3.B)
                end)
                coloredCount = coloredCount + 1
            end
        elseif (d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture")) and not d:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart") then
            if matchesGroup(d) or (d.Parent and matchesGroup(d.Parent)) then
                pcall(function()
                    d.Color3 = c3
                    if texOverride then d.Texture = texOverride end
                end)
                coloredCount = coloredCount + 1
            end
        elseif d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("Trail") then
            if not (d.Parent and d.Parent:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart")) and matchesGroup(d) then
                pcall(function() d.Color = ColorSequence.new(c3) end)
                coloredCount = coloredCount + 1
            end
        elseif d:IsA("Light") then
            if not (d.Parent and d.Parent:GetAttribute("RH_IsSkinPart")) and matchesGroup(d) then
                pcall(function() d.Color = c3 end)
                coloredCount = coloredCount + 1
            end
        end
    end

    -- Sync Configuration/Colors values if present inside the model
    local cfgColors = m:FindFirstChild("Colors", true)
    if cfgColors then
        for _, cVal in ipairs(cfgColors:GetChildren()) do
            local gn = tonumber(tostring(cVal.Name):match("%d+"))
            if gn == group then
                if cVal:IsA("Color3Value") then
                    pcall(function() cVal.Value = c3 end)
                elseif cVal:IsA("StringValue") then
                    pcall(function()
                        cVal:SetAttribute("Color3", string.format("%.3f,%.3f,%.3f", c3.R, c3.G, c3.B))
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if coloredCount == 0 and group == 2 and not hasNumbers then
        local parts = {}
        for _, p in ipairs(allTargets) do
            if p:IsA("BasePart") and not isHelperSet[p.Name] and not RHRigColor.isSkinPart(p) then
                table.insert(parts, p)
            end
        end
        table.sort(parts, function(a, b)
            return (a.Size.X * a.Size.Y * a.Size.Z) < (b.Size.X * b.Size.Y * b.Size.Z)
        end)
        local half = math.max(1, math.floor(#parts / 2))
        for i = 1, half do
            if parts[i] then
                colorSinglePart(parts[i])
                coloredCount = coloredCount + 1
            end
        end
    end
    return coloredCount > 0
end

enforceHiddenBodyParts = function()
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return end
    local bakedFolder = getBakedFolder(false)
    local prevFolder = getPreviewFolder(false)

    if bakedFolder or prevFolder then
        for slotKey, partsMap in pairs(hiddenBodyPartsBySlot) do
            local active = (bakedFolder and bakedFolder:FindFirstChild(slotKey))
                        or (prevFolder and type(slotKey) == "string" and slotKey:sub(1, 8) == "Preview_" and prevFolder:FindFirstChild(slotKey:sub(9)))
            if active then
                for partName, _ in pairs(partsMap) do
                    if partName ~= "HumanoidRootPart" and partName ~= "Head" then
                        local limb = char:FindFirstChild(partName)
                        if limb and limb:IsA("BasePart") and limb.Transparency < 0.99 then
                            limb.Transparency = 1
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for _, folder in ipairs({prevFolder, bakedFolder}) do
            if folder then
                for _, m in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    if m:GetAttribute("RH_HasColoredSkin") then
                        RHRigColor.applySkinToModel(m, char)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    RHRigColor.syncHipHeight(char)
end

restoreUnhiddenBodyParts = function(removedPartsMap)
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return end
    local bakedFolder = getBakedFolder(false)
    local prevFolder = getPreviewFolder(false)

    if removedPartsMap then
        for partName, _ in pairs(removedPartsMap) do
            if partName ~= "HumanoidRootPart" and partName ~= "Head" then
                local stillHidden = false
                for slotKey, partsMap in pairs(hiddenBodyPartsBySlot) do
                    local active = (bakedFolder and bakedFolder:FindFirstChild(slotKey))
                                or (prevFolder and type(slotKey) == "string" and slotKey:sub(1, 8) == "Preview_" and prevFolder:FindFirstChild(slotKey:sub(9)))
                    if active and partsMap[partName] then
                        stillHidden = true
                        break
                    end
                end
                if not stillHidden then
                    local limb = char:FindFirstChild(partName)
                    if limb and limb:IsA("BasePart") then
                        limb.Transparency = 0
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    RHRigColor.syncHipHeight(char)
end

local function registerDefaultAvatar()
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return end
    for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        defaultAvatarInstances[d] = true
    end
end
pcall(registerDefaultAvatar)
if lp.Character then bindHrpAntiAnchor(lp.Character) end

local function deepClone(inst)
    if not inst then return nil end
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        inst.Archivable = true
        for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do
            d.Archivable = true
        end
        return inst:Clone()
    end)
    return ok and result or nil
end

local WornItemsMod = nil
pcall(function()
    local rhUtil = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RHUtil")
    local chUtil = rhUtil and rhUtil:FindFirstChild("Character")
    local wiMod = chUtil and chUtil:FindFirstChild("WornItems")
    if wiMod then WornItemsMod = require(wiMod) end
end)

local function normalizeRhCategory(rawCat)
    local c = tostring(rawCat or ""):lower()
    if c:find("heel") or c:find("shoe") or c == "mermaid" then return "Heels"
    elseif c:find("skirt") then return "Skirts"
    elseif c:find("bodice") or c:find("corset") or c == "torso" then return "Bodices"
    elseif c:find("wing") then return "Wings"
    elseif c:find("halo") then return "Halos"
    else return "Accessories" end
end

local function getEquippedFolders()
    local list = {}
    local seen = {}
    local es = workspace:FindFirstChild("EquippedStorage")
    local char = lp.Character
    local categoryMap = {
        Heels = "Heels", HeelMorphs = "Heels", Shoes = "Heels", Mermaid = "Heels",
        Skirts = "Skirts", Skirt = "Skirts",
        Bodices = "Bodices", Corsets = "Bodices", Torso = "Bodices",
        Accessories = "Accessories", Hair = "Accessories",
        Wings = "Wings", Halos = "Halos"
    }

    local function pushFolder(pf, normCat)
        if pf and not seen[pf] then
            seen[pf] = true
            table.insert(list, {folder = pf, category = normCat or "Accessories"})
        end
    end

    if es then
        for _, cf in ipairs(es:GetChildren()) do
            local pf = cf:FindFirstChild(lp.Name) or cf:FindFirstChild(tostring(lp.UserId))
            if pf then
                pushFolder(pf, categoryMap[cf.Name] or normalizeRhCategory(cf.Name))
            end
        end
    end

    if char then
        local wi = char:FindFirstChild((WornItemsMod and WornItemsMod.FolderName) or "WornItems")
        if wi then
            for _, cf in ipairs(wi:GetChildren()) do
                local pf = cf:FindFirstChild(lp.Name) or cf:FindFirstChild(tostring(lp.UserId))
                if pf then
                    pushFolder(pf, categoryMap[cf.Name] or normalizeRhCategory(cf.Name))
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if WornItemsMod and WornItemsMod.Folders then
        pcall(function()
            for _, pf in ipairs(WornItemsMod.Folders(lp)) do
                local pName = pf.Parent and pf.Parent.Name or ""
                pushFolder(pf, categoryMap[pName] or normalizeRhCategory(pName))
            end
        end)
    end

    return list
end

-- Strictly identify base avatar body parts and hair to prevent hair duplication
local function isBaseAvatarObject(inst)
    if not inst then return true end
    if defaultAvatarInstances[inst] then return true end
    if inst:GetAttribute("RH_BakedLayer") or inst:GetAttribute("RH_ClientCloset") or inst:GetAttribute("RH_PreviewStage") or inst:GetAttribute("RH_PreviewItem") then return true end
    local bf = getBakedFolder(false)
    if bf and (inst == bf or inst:IsDescendantOf(bf)) then return true end
    local pf = getPreviewFolder(false)
    if pf and (inst == pf or inst:IsDescendantOf(pf)) then return true end
    if inst.Name == "Humanoid" or inst.Name == "Animate" or inst.Name == "Head" then return true end
    if standardBodyParts[inst.Name] then return true end

    -- Check if it's avatar hair (Accessory, Model, Folder, or BasePart with HairAttachment or hair keywords)
    local char = lp.Character
    local inEquippedStorage = workspace:FindFirstChild("EquippedStorage") and inst:IsDescendantOf(workspace.EquippedStorage)
    if not inEquippedStorage then
        if inst:IsA("Accessory") or (char and inst.Parent == char and (inst:IsA("Model") or inst:IsA("Folder"))) then
            if inst:FindFirstChild("HairAttachment", true) or inst:FindFirstChild("Hair", true) then
                return true
            end
            local ln = inst.Name:lower()
            if ln:find("hair") or ln:find("wig") or ln:find("bangs") or ln:find("curl")
               or ln:find("ponytail") or ln:find("pigtail") or ln:find("afro") or ln:find("braid")
               or ln:find("locs") or ln:find("bun") or ln:find("fade") or ln:find("extension") then
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    if inst.Name == "Handle" and inst.Parent and inst.Parent:IsA("Accessory") then
        if inst:FindFirstChild("HairAttachment") then return true end
    end

    return false
end

-- Find all actual Royale High clothing pieces attached to avatar
local function findRoyaleHighItemsOnAvatar()
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return {} end
    local itemsFound = {}
    local seen = {}

    local function addItem(inst)
        if not inst or seen[inst] then return end
        if isBaseAvatarObject(inst) then return end
        seen[inst] = true
        table.insert(itemsFound, inst)
    end

    -- 1. Scan EquippedStorage & WornItems folders
    for _, info in ipairs(getEquippedFolders()) do
        for _, child in ipairs(info.folder:GetChildren()) do
            addItem(child)
        end
    end

    -- 2. Scan Character hierarchy for custom models/folders/items
    for _, d in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if not isBaseAvatarObject(d) and d.Name ~= "RH_BakedWardrobe" then
            if d:IsA("Model") or d:IsA("Folder") or d:IsA("Accessory") or d:IsA("MeshPart") or d:IsA("Tool") then
                addItem(d)
            end
        end
    end

    -- 3. Scan welds connected to character rig limbs
    local charLimbs = {}
    for _, limb in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if limb:IsA("BasePart") and standardBodyParts[limb.Name] then
            charLimbs[limb] = true
        end
    end

    local searchRoots = { char }
    local es = workspace:FindFirstChild("EquippedStorage")
    if es then table.insert(searchRoots, es) end

    for _, root in ipairs(searchRoots) do
        for _, w in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            if (w:IsA("Weld") or w:IsA("WeldConstraint") or w:IsA("Motor6D")) then
                local otherPart = nil
                if charLimbs[w.Part0] and w.Part1 and not charLimbs[w.Part1] then
                    otherPart = w.Part1
                elseif charLimbs[w.Part1] and w.Part0 and not charLimbs[w.Part0] then
                    otherPart = w.Part0
                end

                if otherPart and not isBaseAvatarObject(otherPart) then
                    local container = otherPart
                    while container.Parent
                          and container.Parent ~= char
                          and container.Parent ~= workspace
                          and container.Parent ~= es
                          and not container.Parent:IsA("Workspace") do
                        if container.Parent.Name == lp.Name or container.Parent.Name == tostring(lp.UserId) then
                            break
                        end
                        container = container.Parent
                    end
                    addItem(container)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return itemsFound
end

local function countBaseParts(inst)
    if not inst then return 0 end
    if inst:IsA("BasePart") then return 1 end
    local n = 0
    for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") then
            n = n + 1
        end
    end
    return n
end

local function isItemWeldedToChar(inst, char)
    if not inst or not char then return false end
    local charLimbs = {}
    for _, limb in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if limb:IsA("BasePart") and standardBodyParts[limb.Name] then
            charLimbs[limb] = true
        end
    end
    for _, j in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do
        if j:IsA("Weld") or j:IsA("WeldConstraint") or j:IsA("Motor6D") then
            if (j.Part0 and charLimbs[j.Part0]) or (j.Part1 and charLimbs[j.Part1]) then
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    for _, j in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if j:IsA("Weld") or j:IsA("WeldConstraint") or j:IsA("Motor6D") then
            if j.Part0 and j.Part1 then
                if (charLimbs[j.Part0] and (j.Part1 == inst or j.Part1:IsDescendantOf(inst)))
                or (charLimbs[j.Part1] and (j.Part0 == inst or j.Part0:IsDescendantOf(inst))) then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function isNearChar(inst, char, maxDist)
    local hrp = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
    if not inst or not hrp then return false end
    local pos = hrp.Position
    if inst:IsA("BasePart") then
        return (inst.Position - pos).Magnitude <= (maxDist or 25)
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") then
            if (d.Position - pos).Magnitude <= (maxDist or 25) then
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function waitForItemReady(inst, char, maxSec)
    if not inst or not char then return false end
    local lastCount = countBaseParts(inst)
    local stableChecks = 0
    local settleStart = tick()
    local limit = maxSec or 2.2
    while tick() - settleStart < limit do
        task.wait(0.08)
        if not inst.Parent then return false end
        local curCount = countBaseParts(inst)
        local weldedOrNear = isItemWeldedToChar(inst, char) or isNearChar(inst, char, 25)
        if curCount > 0 and curCount == lastCount and (weldedOrNear or (tick() - settleStart) >= 1.4) then
            stableChecks = stableChecks + 1
            if stableChecks >= 2 then
                return true
            end
        else
            lastCount = curCount
            stableChecks = 0
        end
    end
    return countBaseParts(inst) > 0
end


-- Find a specific newly spawned Royale High item on the avatar or in EquippedStorage (must have >=1 BasePart!)
local function findSpecificItemOnAvatar(itemName, cat, beforeSet)
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return nil, nil end
    local cleanTarget = (itemName or ""):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")

    local function matchesName(inst)
        if not inst then return false end
        local n = inst.Name:lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
        if cleanTarget ~= "" and (n == cleanTarget or n:find(cleanTarget, 1, true) or cleanTarget:find(n, 1, true)) then
            return true
        end
        for _, vName in ipairs({"HeelName", "ItemName", "AssetName", "SkirtName", "BodiceName"}) do
            local sv = inst:FindFirstChild(vName, true)
            if sv and sv:IsA("StringValue") then
                local vn = sv.Value:lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
                if cleanTarget ~= "" and (vn == cleanTarget or vn:find(cleanTarget, 1, true) or cleanTarget:find(vn, 1, true)) then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
        local attr = inst:GetAttribute("RH_ItemName")
        if type(attr) == "string" then
            local an = attr:lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
            if cleanTarget ~= "" and (an == cleanTarget or an:find(cleanTarget, 1, true) or cleanTarget:find(an, 1, true)) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    -- 1. Check EquippedStorage & WornItems first
    for _, info in ipairs(getEquippedFolders()) do
        for _, child in ipairs(info.folder:GetChildren()) do
            if not isBaseAvatarObject(child) and countBaseParts(child) > 0 then
                if matchesName(child) then
                    return child, info.category
                end
                if beforeSet and not beforeSet[child] and (not cat or info.category == cat) then
                    return child, info.category
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 2. Check Character direct children (excluding RH_BakedWardrobe)
    for _, d in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if not isBaseAvatarObject(d) and d.Name ~= "RH_BakedWardrobe" then
            if (d:IsA("Model") or d:IsA("Folder") or d:IsA("Accessory") or d:IsA("MeshPart") or d:IsA("Tool")) and countBaseParts(d) > 0 then
                if matchesName(d) then
                    return d, cat
                end
                if beforeSet and not beforeSet[d] then
                    return d, cat
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 3. Universal Weld-to-Limb Scan across char & EquippedStorage (catches ANY folder name Royale High uses!)
    local charLimbs = {}
    for _, limb in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if limb:IsA("BasePart") and standardBodyParts[limb.Name] then
            charLimbs[limb] = true
        end
    end
    local es = workspace:FindFirstChild("EquippedStorage")
    local searchRoots = { char }
    if es then table.insert(searchRoots, es) end

    for _, root in ipairs(searchRoots) do
        for _, w in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            if w:IsA("Weld") or w:IsA("WeldConstraint") or w:IsA("Motor6D") then
                local otherPart = nil
                if charLimbs[w.Part0] and w.Part1 and not charLimbs[w.Part1] then
                    otherPart = w.Part1
                elseif charLimbs[w.Part1] and w.Part0 and not charLimbs[w.Part0] then
                    otherPart = w.Part0
                end
                if otherPart and not isBaseAvatarObject(otherPart) then
                    local bakedFolder = getBakedFolder(false)
                    if not (bakedFolder and (otherPart == bakedFolder or otherPart:IsDescendantOf(bakedFolder))) then
                        local container = otherPart
                        while container.Parent
                              and container.Parent ~= char
                              and container.Parent ~= workspace
                              and container.Parent ~= es
                              and not container.Parent:IsA("Workspace") do
                            if container.Parent.Name == lp.Name or container.Parent.Name == tostring(lp.UserId) then
                                break
                            end
                            container = container.Parent
                        end
                        if not isBaseAvatarObject(container) and container.Name ~= "RH_BakedWardrobe" and countBaseParts(container) > 0 then
                            if matchesName(container) or (beforeSet and not beforeSet[container]) then
                                return container, cat
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return nil, nil
end

-- 1:1 Single-Clone Layer Baker Engine: Clones exactly 1 instance per slot, matching relative CFrames & welds 1:1
local function resolveCharLimb(char, limbName)
    if not char or not limbName or limbName == "" then return nil end
    local direct = char:FindFirstChild(limbName)
    if direct and direct:IsA("BasePart") then return direct end
    local r6Map = {
        UpperTorso = "Torso", LowerTorso = "Torso",
        LeftUpperArm = "Left Arm", LeftLowerArm = "Left Arm", LeftHand = "Left Arm",
        RightUpperArm = "Right Arm", RightLowerArm = "Right Arm", RightHand = "Right Arm",
        LeftUpperLeg = "Left Leg", LeftLowerLeg = "Left Leg", LeftFoot = "Left Leg",
        RightUpperLeg = "Right Leg", RightLowerLeg = "Right Leg", RightFoot = "Right Leg",
        Middle = "Head",
    }
    local mapped = r6Map[limbName]
    if mapped then
        local m = char:FindFirstChild(mapped)
        if m and m:IsA("BasePart") then return m end
    end
    return nil
end

local function bakeItemToAvatar(sourceItem, category, customSlotKey, itemData, previewOnly)
    if not sourceItem then return nil end
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    if countBaseParts(sourceItem) == 0 then return nil end

    local cat = category or "Accessories"
    local slotKey = nil
    if customSlotKey then
        slotKey = customSlotKey
    elseif cat == "Heels" or cat == "Skirts" or cat == "Bodices" or cat == "Wings" then
        slotKey = cat
    elseif cat == "Halos" then
        local haloName = (itemData and (itemData.name or itemData.assetName)) or sourceItem.Name
        local cleanH = tostring(haloName):gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
        slotKey = "Halo_" .. (cleanH ~= "" and cleanH or tostring(tick()):gsub("%.", ""))
    else
        local accName = (itemData and (itemData.name or itemData.assetName)) or sourceItem.Name
        if accName == "Accessory" or accName == "Model" or accName == "Folder" or accName == "Part" then
            local sub = sourceItem:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpecialMesh", true) or sourceItem:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("MeshPart", true)
            if sub and sub.Name ~= "MeshPart" and sub.Name ~= "Mesh" then
                accName = sub.Name
            end
        end
        local cleanA = tostring(accName):gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
        slotKey = "Acc_" .. (cleanA ~= "" and cleanA or tostring(tick()):gsub("%.", ""))
    end

    local charLimbs = {}
    for _, limb in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if limb:IsA("BasePart") and standardBodyParts[limb.Name] then
            charLimbs[limb] = true
        end
    end

    -- Multi-limb detection: tag EVERY part inside sourceItem with the EXACT character limb it was welded to BEFORE cloning!
    local detectedLimb = nil
    local taggedTempParts = {}
    local function recordPartLimbHint(part, limb)
        if not part or not limb then return end
        if not detectedLimb then detectedLimb = limb end
        if not part:GetAttribute("RH_LimbHint") then
            part:SetAttribute("RH_LimbHint", limb.Name)
            table.insert(taggedTempParts, part)
        end
    end

    local jointContainers = { sourceItem, char }
    for _, container in ipairs(jointContainers) do
        for _, j in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
            if j:IsA("Weld") or j:IsA("WeldConstraint") or j:IsA("Motor6D") then
                local p0, p1 = j.Part0, j.Part1
                if p0 and p1 then
                    if charLimbs[p0] and (p1 == sourceItem or p1:IsDescendantOf(sourceItem)) then
                        recordPartLimbHint(p1, p0)
                    elseif charLimbs[p1] and (p0 == sourceItem or p0:IsDescendantOf(sourceItem)) then
                        recordPartLimbHint(p0, p1)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local bakedFolder = previewOnly and getPreviewFolder(true) or getBakedFolder(true)

    -- Strict single-clone per slot: destroy old clone for THIS specific slot/accessory
    local existing = bakedFolder:FindFirstChild(slotKey)
    if existing then
        pcall(function() existing:Destroy() end)
    end
    if not previewOnly then
        bakedLayersBackup[slotKey] = nil
    end

    -- Deep clone source item, then remove temporary limb hint attributes from sourceItem
    local ok, clone = pcall(function()
        sourceItem.Archivable = true
        for _, d in ipairs(sourceItem:GetDescendants()) do d.Archivable = true end
        return sourceItem:Clone()
    end)
    for _, tp in ipairs(taggedTempParts) do
        if tp and tp.Parent then pcall(function() tp:SetAttribute("RH_LimbHint", nil) end) end
    end
    if not ok or not clone then return nil end

    -- If sourceItem was a Tool, convert to Model so it wears passively without equipping as a tool
    if clone:IsA("Tool") then
        local m = Instance.new("Model")
        m.Name = clone.Name
        for _, ch in ipairs(clone:GetChildren()) do
            if not ch:IsA("Script") and not ch:IsA("LocalScript") then
                ch.Parent = m
            end
        end
        clone:Destroy()
        clone = m
    end

    local imgToStore = (itemData and itemData.img and itemData.img ~= "") and itemData.img or ""
    if imgToStore == "" and findItemImage then
        imgToStore = findItemImage(itemData and itemData.name or sourceItem.Name, cat)
    end

    clone.Name = slotKey
    if previewOnly then
        clone:SetAttribute("RH_PreviewItem", true)
    else
        clone:SetAttribute("RH_BakedLayer", true)
    end
    clone:SetAttribute("RH_Category", cat)
    clone:SetAttribute("RH_SlotKey", slotKey)
    clone:SetAttribute("RH_ItemName", (itemData and itemData.name) or sourceItem.Name)
    clone:SetAttribute("RH_Image", imgToStore)
    clone:SetAttribute("RH_Price", (itemData and itemData.price) or "")

    -- Preserve internal Motor6Ds, Bones, AnimationControllers, & VFX; remove only joints pointing outside clone
    for _, j in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
        if j:IsA("BodyMover") or j:IsA("Script") or j:IsA("LocalScript") then
            pcall(function() j:Destroy() end)
        elseif j:IsA("JointInstance") or j:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
            local p0ok = j.Part0 and (j.Part0 == clone or j.Part0:IsDescendantOf(clone))
            local p1ok = j.Part1 and (j.Part1 == clone or j.Part1:IsDescendantOf(clone))
            if not (p0ok and p1ok) then
                pcall(function() j:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
    end

    local zeroPhys = PhysicalProperties.new(0.01, 0, 0, 0, 0)
    local function prepPart(p)
        p.CanCollide = false
        p.CanTouch   = false
        p.CanQuery   = false
        p.Anchored   = false
        p.Massless   = true
        pcall(function() p.CustomPhysicalProperties = zeroPhys end)
    end

    -- Parent clone into safe workspace closet FIRST so Welds activate immediately!
    clone.Parent = bakedFolder

    local function weldRigid(part0, part1, weldName)
        local savedC0 = part1:GetAttribute("RH_SavedC0")
        local relC0
        if typeof(savedC0) == "CFrame" and part1:GetAttribute("RH_SavedPart0") == part0.Name then
            relC0 = savedC0
            part1.CFrame = part0.CFrame * relC0
        else
            relC0 = part0.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(part1.CFrame)
            part1:SetAttribute("RH_SavedC0", relC0)
            part1:SetAttribute("RH_SavedPart0", part0.Name)
        end
        local w = Instance.new("Weld")
        w.Name = weldName or "RH_RigidWeld"
        w.Part0 = part0
        w.Part1 = part1
        w.C0 = relC0
        w.C1 = CFrame.new()
        w:SetAttribute("RH_SavedPart0", part0.Name)
        w:SetAttribute("RH_SavedC0", relC0)
        w.Parent = part1
        return w
    end

    -- Build connected-component adjacency across ALL surviving internal joints inside clone
    local adj = {}
    for _, j in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
        if j:IsA("JointInstance") or j:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
            local a, b = j.Part0, j.Part1
            if a and b and a:IsA("BasePart") and b:IsA("BasePart") then
                adj[a] = adj[a] or {}
                adj[b] = adj[b] or {}
                table.insert(adj[a], b)
                table.insert(adj[b], a)
            end
        end
    end

    local attachedSet = {}
    local function markComponent(startPart)
        if not startPart or attachedSet[startPart] then return end
        local q = { startPart }
        attachedSet[startPart] = true
        local head = 1
        while head <= #q do
            local cur = q[head]; head = head + 1
            local nbrs = adj[cur]
            if nbrs then
                for _, nb in ipairs(nbrs) do
                    if not attachedSet[nb] then
                        attachedSet[nb] = true
                        table.insert(q, nb)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")

    -- Measure base avatar foot bottom Y & live HipHeight BEFORE welding Heels!
    local baseFootBottomY = nil
    local itemLowestBottomY = nil
    if cat == "Heels" then
        for _, ln in ipairs(legPartNames) do
            local lpPart = char:FindFirstChild(ln)
            if lpPart and lpPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                local bY = lpPart.Position.Y - (lpPart.Size.Y * 0.5)
                if not baseFootBottomY or bY < baseFootBottomY then baseFootBottomY = bY end
            end
        end
    end

    if cat == "Heels" and hrp then
        RHRigColor.snapshotOrigHipHeight(char)
        local leftLimb = char:FindFirstChild("LeftLowerLeg") or char:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg") or char:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or hrp
        local rightLimb = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg") or char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg") or char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") or hrp

        local leftParts, rightParts = {}, {}
        local function sortHeelPart(p)
            prepPart(p)
            if not HELPER_TRANSPARENT_PARTS[p.Name] and p.Transparency < 0.99 then
                local py = p.Position.Y - (p.Size.Y * 0.5)
                if not itemLowestBottomY or py < itemLowestBottomY then itemLowestBottomY = py end
            end
            local side = p:GetAttribute("RH_HeelSide")
            if side == "L" then
                table.insert(leftParts, p)
            elseif side == "R" then
                table.insert(rightParts, p)
            else
                local isL = hrp.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(p.Position).X < 0
                p:SetAttribute("RH_HeelSide", isL and "L" or "R")
                table.insert(isL and leftParts or rightParts, p)
            end
        end

        if clone:IsA("BasePart") then
            sortHeelPart(clone)
        else
            for _, p in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") then sortHeelPart(p) end
            end
        end

        -- Avoid cross-joint lock when internal rigid welds span UpperLeg <-> LowerLeg <-> Foot!
        for _, j in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
            if j:IsA("Weld") or j:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
                local a, b = j.Part0, j.Part1
                if a and b and a:IsA("BasePart") and b:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local ha = a:GetAttribute("RH_LimbHint") or a:GetAttribute("RH_SavedPart0")
                    local hb = b:GetAttribute("RH_LimbHint") or b:GetAttribute("RH_SavedPart0")
                    if ha and hb and ha ~= hb then
                        pcall(function() j:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- Bind parts with explicit multi-limb hints first (thighs / calves / feet animate naturally!)
        for _, list in ipairs({leftParts, rightParts}) do
            for _, p in ipairs(list) do
                if not attachedSet[p] then
                    local hint = p:GetAttribute("RH_LimbHint") or p:GetAttribute("RH_SavedPart0")
                    local targetL = (hint and resolveCharLimb(char, hint)) or nil
                    if targetL and targetL:IsDescendantOf(char) then
                        weldRigid(targetL, p, "RH_HeelLimbWeld")
                        markComponent(p)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function rigBoot(partsList, targetLimb)
