            local unattached = {}
            for _, p in ipairs(partsList) do
                if not attachedSet[p] then table.insert(unattached, p) end
            end
            if #unattached == 0 or not targetLimb then return end
            local root = unattached[1]
            local maxVol = -1
            for _, p in ipairs(unattached) do
                local vol = p.Size.X * p.Size.Y * p.Size.Z
                if vol > maxVol then maxVol = vol; root = p end
            end
            weldRigid(targetLimb, root, "RH_BootRootWeld")
            markComponent(root)
            -- Weld ANY remaining disconnected sub-island in this boot to root!
            for _, p in ipairs(unattached) do
                if not attachedSet[p] then
                    weldRigid(root, p, "RH_BootSubWeld")
                    markComponent(p)
                end
            end
        end

        rigBoot(leftParts, leftLimb)
        rigBoot(rightParts, rightLimb)
    else
        local fallbackLimb = detectedLimb
        if not fallbackLimb then
            if cat == "Skirts" then
                fallbackLimb = char:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or hrp
            elseif cat == "Bodices" or cat == "Wings" then
                fallbackLimb = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or hrp
            elseif cat == "Halos" then
                fallbackLimb = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or hrp
            else
                local att = clone:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Attachment", true)
                if att then
                    local an = att.Name:lower()
                    if an:find("hat") or an:find("face") or an:find("hair") or an:find("head") then
                        fallbackLimb = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    elseif an:find("waist") then
                        fallbackLimb = char:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                    elseif an:find("rightgrip") or an:find("righthand") then
                        fallbackLimb = char:FindFirstChild("RightHand") or char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
                    elseif an:find("leftgrip") or an:find("lefthand") then
                        fallbackLimb = char:FindFirstChild("LeftHand") or char:FindFirstChild("Left Arm")
                    elseif an:find("rightshoulder") then
                        fallbackLimb = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm") or char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
                    elseif an:find("leftshoulder") then
                        fallbackLimb = char:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm") or char:FindFirstChild("Left Arm")
                    elseif an:find("body") or an:find("back") or an:find("neck") or an:find("collar") then
                        fallbackLimb = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local allParts = {}
        if clone:IsA("BasePart") then
            prepPart(clone)
            table.insert(allParts, clone)
        else
            for _, p in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                    prepPart(p)
                    table.insert(allParts, p)
                end
            end
        end

        if #allParts > 0 then
            -- First bind any part with an exact RH_LimbHint directly to its matching limb (preserving sleeves/multi-limb articulation!)
            local primaryRoot = nil
            for _, p in ipairs(allParts) do
                local hint = p:GetAttribute("RH_LimbHint") or p:GetAttribute("RH_SavedPart0")
                if type(hint) == "string" and hint ~= "" and not attachedSet[p] then
                    local targetL = resolveCharLimb(char, hint)
                    if targetL then
                        weldRigid(targetL, p, "RH_LimbWeld")
                        markComponent(p)
                        if not primaryRoot then primaryRoot = p end
                    end
                end
            end

            -- Fallback root if not all parts reached yet
            local root = primaryRoot
                      or (clone:IsA("Model") and clone.PrimaryPart)
                      or clone:FindFirstChild("Middle")
                      or clone:FindFirstChild("Center")
                      or clone:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                      or allParts[1]

            if not fallbackLimb then
                local bestDist = math.huge
                for limb, _ in pairs(charLimbs) do
                    if limb.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                        local dist = (limb.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist < bestDist then
                            bestDist = dist
                            fallbackLimb = limb
                        end
                    end
                end
                fallbackLimb = fallbackLimb or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or hrp
            end

            if not attachedSet[root] and fallbackLimb then
                weldRigid(fallbackLimb, root, "RH_UnifiedRootWeld")
                markComponent(root)
            end

            -- Connect EVERY remaining disconnected sub-island cleanly to root so nothing EVER falls into the void!
            for _, p in ipairs(allParts) do
                if not attachedSet[p] then
                    weldRigid(root, p, "RH_UnifiedSubWeld")
                    markComponent(p)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if countBaseParts(clone) == 0 then
        pcall(function() clone:Destroy() end)
        return nil
    end

    -- Snapshot & lock base avatar body parts hidden ONLY by this morph (preserve Hands!)
    local hiddenMap = {}
    for _, limb in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if limb:IsA("BasePart") and standardBodyParts[limb.Name] and limb.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" and limb.Name ~= "Head" then
            if limb.Transparency >= 0.95 then
                hiddenMap[limb.Name] = true
            end
        end
    end
    local armSleeveNames = {
        "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "Left Arm",
        "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "Right Arm"
    }
    local handPartNames = { "LeftHand", "RightHand" }
    local torsoPartNames = { "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Torso" }

    if cat == "Heels" then
        for _, lName in ipairs(legPartNames) do hiddenMap[lName] = true end
    end
    if sourceItem:FindFirstChild("HideTorso", true) then
        for _, x in ipairs(torsoPartNames) do hiddenMap[x] = true end
    else
        if sourceItem:FindFirstChild("HideUpperTorso", true) or sourceItem:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso", true) then
            hiddenMap["UpperTorso"] = true
            hiddenMap["Torso"] = true
        end
        if sourceItem:FindFirstChild("HideLowerTorso", true) or sourceItem:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso", true) then
            hiddenMap["LowerTorso"] = true
        end
        if sourceItem:FindFirstChild("Torso", true) then
            for _, x in ipairs(torsoPartNames) do hiddenMap[x] = true end
        end
    end
    for _, aName in ipairs(armSleeveNames) do
        if sourceItem:FindFirstChild(aName, true) or sourceItem:FindFirstChild("HideArms", true) then
            hiddenMap[aName] = true
        end
    end
    for _, hName in ipairs(handPartNames) do
        if sourceItem:FindFirstChild("HideHands", true) then
            hiddenMap[hName] = true
        end
    end
    for _, lName in ipairs(legPartNames) do
        if sourceItem:FindFirstChild(lName, true) or sourceItem:FindFirstChild("HideLegs", true) then
            hiddenMap[lName] = true
        end
    end

    -- Format MultiColor channels & tint skin parts (e.g. coloredleg / SurfaceAppearance)
    RHRigColor.formatModel(clone, nil)
    RHRigColor.applySkinToModel(clone, char)

    -- Preserve / calculate target HipHeight for Heels or HipHeight-altering morphs
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local existingHH = clone:GetAttribute("RH_TargetHipHeight")
    local targetHH = type(existingHH) == "number" and existingHH or nil
    if not targetHH then
        local hhObj = clone:FindFirstChild("HipHeight", true) or sourceItem:FindFirstChild("HipHeight", true)
        local hhAttr = clone:GetAttribute("HipHeight") or sourceItem:GetAttribute("HipHeight")
        if hhObj and (hhObj:IsA("NumberValue") or hhObj:IsA("IntValue")) and hhObj.Value > 0.5 then
            targetHH = hhObj.Value
        elseif type(hhAttr) == "number" and hhAttr > 0.5 then
            targetHH = hhAttr
        elseif cat == "Heels" and hum then
            local baseHH = RHRigColor.origHipHeight or 2
            if hum.HipHeight > baseHH + 0.05 then
                targetHH = hum.HipHeight
            elseif baseFootBottomY and itemLowestBottomY and itemLowestBottomY < (baseFootBottomY - 0.12) then
                targetHH = baseHH + (baseFootBottomY - itemLowestBottomY)
            end
        end
    end
    if targetHH then
        clone:SetAttribute("RH_TargetHipHeight", targetHH)
    end

    if next(hiddenMap) ~= nil then
        local hideKey = (previewOnly and "Preview_" or "") .. slotKey
        hiddenBodyPartsBySlot[hideKey] = hiddenMap
    end
    enforceHiddenBodyParts()

    -- Re-apply any saved user colors (if switching toggles after picking colors)
    if savedColorsBySlot[slotKey] then
        for gIdx, cEntry in pairs(savedColorsBySlot[slotKey]) do
            RHRigColor.applyColorToModel(clone, char, cEntry.c3, gIdx, cEntry.tex)
        end
    end

    -- Replay internal AnimationController animations 1:1
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            local animCtrl = clone:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("AnimationController", true)
            local animator = animCtrl and (animCtrl:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Animator") or Instance.new("Animator", animCtrl))
            if animator then
                for _, anim in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
                    if anim:IsA("Animation") and anim.AnimationId ~= "" then
                        pcall(function()
                            local track = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
                            track.Looped = true
                            track:Play()
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end)

    if not previewOnly then
        bakedLayersBackup[slotKey] = deepClone(clone)
        local cKey = ((cat or "") .. "|" .. ((itemData and (itemData.assetName or itemData.name)) or sourceItem.Name)):lower()
        clientItemCache[cKey] = deepClone(clone)
    end
    return clone
end

-- Lock avatar's current look into single-layer baked slots
local isBakingDebounce = false
local function lockCurrentAvatarLook(catHint, itemData)
    if isBakingDebounce then return 0 end

    local items = findRoyaleHighItemsOnAvatar()
    if #items == 0 then return 0 end

    isBakingDebounce = true
    local count = 0
    for _, item in ipairs(items) do
        local cat = catHint or "Accessories"
        if item.Parent and item.Parent.Parent and (item.Parent.Parent.Name == "EquippedStorage" or item.Parent.Parent.Name == "WornItems") then
            cat = normalizeRhCategory(item.Parent.Name)
        end
        local b = bakeItemToAvatar(item, cat, nil, itemData)
        if b then count = count + 1 end
    end

    task.delay(0.3, function()
        isBakingDebounce = false
    end)
    return count
end

-- Unequip a single worn slot
local function unequipWornItem(item)
    if not item then return end
    local char = lp.Character
    local itemName = item.name or ""
    local slotKey  = item.slotKey or ""
    local cat      = item.cat or ""

    -- 1. Remove ONLY this specific item from RH_BakedWardrobe
    if item.bakedInst and item.bakedInst.Parent then
        pcall(function() item.bakedInst:Destroy() end)
    end
    if slotKey ~= "" then
        if activeHaloBobConns and activeHaloBobConns[slotKey] then
            pcall(function() activeHaloBobConns[slotKey]:Disconnect() end)
            activeHaloBobConns[slotKey] = nil
        end
        local bakedFolder = getBakedFolder(false)
        if bakedFolder then
            local b = bakedFolder:FindFirstChild(slotKey)
            if b then pcall(function() b:Destroy() end) end
        end
    end

    -- 2. Clear ONLY this slot's backup table entry
    if slotKey ~= "" and bakedLayersBackup[slotKey] then
        bakedLayersBackup[slotKey] = nil
    elseif cat ~= "" and (cat == "Heels" or cat == "Skirts" or cat == "Bodices" or cat == "Wings") then
        bakedLayersBackup[cat] = nil
    end

    -- 3. Destroy live try-on instance if this item was a live try-on
    local hadLive = (item.liveInst ~= nil)
    if item.liveInst and item.liveInst.Parent then
        pcall(function() item.liveInst:Destroy() end)
    end

    -- 4. Purge ONLY matching item from EquippedStorage / WornItems (guard against empty itemName!)
    if itemName ~= "" and #itemName >= 3 then
        local lowName = itemName:lower()
        for _, info in ipairs(getEquippedFolders()) do
            for _, child in ipairs(info.folder:GetChildren()) do
                local cn = child.Name:lower()
                if cn == lowName or (cn ~= "" and #cn >= 3 and (cn:find(lowName, 1, true) or lowName:find(cn, 1, true))) then
                    pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 5. Purge ONLY matching slot/item directly on Character (never touch other slots!)
    if char and (slotKey ~= "" or itemName ~= "") then
        for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if child.Name ~= "RH_BakedWardrobe" then
                if (slotKey ~= "" and child.Name == slotKey)
                   or (itemName ~= "" and child.Name == itemName)
                   or (slotKey ~= "" and child:GetAttribute("RH_SlotKey") == slotKey) then
                    pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 6. Restore any base body parts hidden ONLY by this slot (if no other active slot still hides them)
    local removedMap = (slotKey ~= "" and hiddenBodyPartsBySlot[slotKey]) or nil
    if slotKey ~= "" then
        hiddenBodyPartsBySlot[slotKey] = nil
    end
    if cat == "Heels" and (slotKey == "Heels" or slotKey == "") then
        removedMap = removedMap or {}
        for _, lName in ipairs(legPartNames) do
            removedMap[lName] = true
        end
        hiddenBodyPartsBySlot["Heels"] = nil
    elseif cat == "Bodices" and (slotKey == "Bodices" or slotKey == "") then
        hiddenBodyPartsBySlot["Bodices"] = nil
    end
    if removedMap then
        restoreUnhiddenBodyParts(removedMap)
    end

    -- 7. Destroy custom wings only if unequipping Wings
    if cat == "Wings" and char then
        for _, n in ipairs({"Wings_TRYON", "CustomWingRight", "CustomWingLeft"}) do
            local w = char:FindFirstChild(n)
            if w then pcall(function() w:Destroy() end) end
        end
    end

    -- 8. Only fire ExitPreview if we were removing a live server try-on item (not when unequipping a single baked clone)
    if hadLive and exitPreviewRemote then
        pcall(function() exitPreviewRemote:FireServer() end)
    end

    -- 9. Release movement and refresh UI
    releasePlayerMovement()
    if UI.updateTabCounts then UI.updateTabCounts() end
    if UI.refreshGrid then UI.refreshGrid("Worn") end
end

-- Clear all baked layers and temporary try-ons, resetting character to normal
local function clearAllWornLayers()
    local char = lp.Character

    for _, info in ipairs(getEquippedFolders()) do
        for _, c in ipairs(info.folder:GetChildren()) do
            pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
        end
    end

    if activeHaloBobConns then
        for sk, conn in pairs(activeHaloBobConns) do
            pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
            activeHaloBobConns[sk] = nil
        end
    end
    local safeBaked = getBakedFolder(false)
    if safeBaked then
        pcall(function() safeBaked:Destroy() end)
    end
    if char then
        local legacyBf = char:FindFirstChild("RH_BakedWardrobe")
        if legacyBf then pcall(function() legacyBf:Destroy() end) end

        for _, c in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if c:GetAttribute("RH_BakedLayer")
               or c:GetAttribute("CustomSavedLayer")
               or c:GetAttribute("RH_ClientLayer")
               or c:GetAttribute("RH_SlotKey")
               or c.Name:find("RH_Baked")
               or c.Name == "Wings_TRYON"
               or c.Name:find("CustomWing") then
                pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
            end
        end

        for partName, _ in pairs(standardBodyParts) do
            if partName ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                local limb = char:FindFirstChild(partName)
                if limb and limb:IsA("BasePart") then
                    limb.Transparency = 0
                end
            end
        end
    end

    pcall(clearPreviewStage)
    keepTogglesOpenUntil = 0
    hiddenBodyPartsBySlot = {}
    bakedLayersBackup = {}
    if char then pcall(function() RHRigColor.syncHipHeight(char) end) end
    if exitPreviewRemote then
        pcall(function() exitPreviewRemote:FireServer() end)
    end
end

-- Get list of all currently worn/baked items for Worn section
local function getWornItemsList()
    local wornList = {}
    local seen = {}
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return wornList end

    local function pushWornEntry(itemName, cat, slotKey, img, price, bakedInst, liveInst)
        itemName = tostring(itemName or slotKey or "Equipped Item")
        cat = tostring(cat or "Accessories")
        slotKey = tostring(slotKey or itemName)
        img = type(img) == "string" and img or ""
        price = tostring(price or "")
        if img == "" and findItemImage then
            pcall(function() img = findItemImage(itemName, cat) or "" end)
        end
        local cleanKey = (cat .. "_" .. itemName):lower():gsub("%s+", "")
        local slotL = slotKey:lower()
        if not seen[slotL] and not seen[cleanKey] then
            seen[slotL] = true
            seen[cleanKey] = true
            table.insert(wornList, {
                name = itemName,
                assetName = itemName,
                cat = cat,
                slotKey = slotKey,
                img = img,
                price = (price ~= "" and price) or "Equipped",
                isWorn = true,
                bakedInst = bakedInst,
                liveInst = liveInst,
            })
        end
    end

    -- 1. Scan safe workspace closet (our primary baked layers)
    local bakedFolder = getBakedFolder(false)
    if bakedFolder then
        for _, clone in ipairs(bakedFolder:GetChildren()) do
            pcall(function()
                local itemName = clone:GetAttribute("RH_ItemName") or clone.Name:gsub("^RH_Baked_", "")
                local cat = clone:GetAttribute("RH_Category") or "Accessories"
                local slotKey = clone:GetAttribute("RH_SlotKey") or clone.Name
                local img = clone:GetAttribute("RH_Image") or ""
                local price = clone:GetAttribute("RH_Price") or ""
                pushWornEntry(itemName, cat, slotKey, img, price, clone, nil)
            end)
        end
    end

    -- 1b. Scan bakedLayersBackup fallback so active/restoring clones always appear in Worn
    for sk, bkp in pairs(bakedLayersBackup) do
        pcall(function()
            local itemName = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_ItemName") or tostring(sk)
            local cat = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_Category") or "Accessories"
            local slotKey = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_SlotKey") or tostring(sk)
            local img = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_Image") or ""
            local price = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_Price") or ""
            local liveClone = bakedFolder and bakedFolder:FindFirstChild(slotKey)
            pushWornEntry(itemName, cat, slotKey, img, price, liveClone or bkp, nil)
        end)
    end

    -- 2. Scan EquippedStorage & WornItems for any live items not yet baked
    pcall(function()
        for _, info in ipairs(getEquippedFolders()) do
            for _, child in ipairs(info.folder:GetChildren()) do
                if not isBaseAvatarObject(child) then
                    local cleanKey = (info.category .. "_" .. child.Name):lower():gsub("%s+", "")
                    if not seen[cleanKey] and not seen[child.Name:lower()] then
                        seen[cleanKey] = true
                        local img = ""
                        if findItemImage then pcall(function() img = findItemImage(child.Name, info.category) or "" end) end
                        table.insert(wornList, {
                            name = child.Name,
                            assetName = child.Name,
                            cat = info.category,
                            slotKey = info.category .. "_" .. child.Name,
                            img = img,
                            price = "Live Try-On",
                            isWorn = true,
                            liveInst = child
                        })
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- 3. Custom wings
    local wingClone = char:FindFirstChild("Wings_TRYON") or char:FindFirstChild("CustomWingRight") or char:FindFirstChild("CustomWingLeft")
    if wingClone and not seen["wings"] then
        seen["wings"] = true
        local wName = wingClone:GetAttribute("RH_ItemName") or "Custom Wings"
        local img = ""
        if findItemImage then pcall(function() img = findItemImage(wName, "Wings") or "" end) end
        table.insert(wornList, {
            name = wName,
            assetName = wName,
            cat = "Wings",
            slotKey = "Wings",
            img = img,
            price = "Equipped Wings",
            isWorn = true,
            bakedInst = wingClone
        })
    end

    return wornList
end

-- Weld-Integrity & Closet Watchdog: immediately restores missing slots AND re-binds broken limb welds!
local isRestoringSlot = {}
local function restoreOrRepairSlot(slotKey, bkp)
    if not slotKey or not bkp or isRestoringSlot[slotKey] then return end
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return end
    isRestoringSlot[slotKey] = true
    pcall(function()
        local bakedFolder = getBakedFolder(true)
        local liveClone = bakedFolder:FindFirstChild(slotKey)
        if not liveClone then
            local bKey = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_CustomB64Key")
            local cat = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_Category") or "Accessories"
            local iName = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_ItemName") or slotKey
            local iImg = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_Image") or ""
            local iPrice = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_Price") or ""
            if bKey and CUSTOM_RBXM_REGISTRY and CUSTOM_RBXM_REGISTRY[bKey] and equipCustomRbxmItemRef then
                equipCustomRbxmItemRef({name = iName, img = iImg, price = iPrice, cat = cat}, CUSTOM_RBXM_REGISTRY[bKey], iName, bKey)
            else
                local clone = deepClone(bkp)
                if clone then
                    bakeItemToAvatar(clone, cat, slotKey, {name = iName, img = iImg, price = iPrice, cat = cat})
                end
            end
        else
            -- Check if Royale High swapped/replaced any Character limb so Weld.Part0 went stale!
            for _, w in ipairs(liveClone:GetDescendants()) do
                if w:IsA("Weld") or w:IsA("WeldConstraint") or w:IsA("Motor6D") then
                    local savedLimbName = w:GetAttribute("RH_SavedPart0")
                    if type(savedLimbName) == "string" and savedLimbName ~= "" then
                        if not w.Part0 or not w.Part0:IsDescendantOf(char) then
                            local targetL = resolveCharLimb(char, savedLimbName)
                            if targetL and w.Part1 and w.Part1:IsA("BasePart") then
                                local savedC0 = w:GetAttribute("RH_SavedC0")
                                w.Part0 = targetL
                                if w:IsA("Weld") and typeof(savedC0) == "CFrame" then
                                    w.C0 = savedC0
                                    w.Part1.CFrame = targetL.CFrame * savedC0
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    isRestoringSlot[slotKey] = nil
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.25) do
        local char = lp.Character
        if char then
            if next(bakedLayersBackup) ~= nil then
                for slotKey, bkp in pairs(bakedLayersBackup) do
                    restoreOrRepairSlot(slotKey, bkp)
                end
            end
            pcall(enforceHiddenBodyParts)
        end
    end
end)

-- Auto-restore baked layers upon character respawn
lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
    RHRigColor.origHipHeight = nil
    RHRigColor.appliedHipHeight = nil
    task.wait(1.0)
    pcall(registerDefaultAvatar)
    newChar:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
    RHRigColor.snapshotOrigHipHeight(newChar)
    for slotKey, bkp in pairs(bakedLayersBackup) do
        pcall(function()
            local bKey = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_CustomB64Key")
            local cat = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_Category") or "Accessories"
            local iName = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_ItemName") or slotKey
            local iImg = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_Image") or ""
            local iPrice = bkp:GetAttribute("RH_Price") or ""
            if bKey and CUSTOM_RBXM_REGISTRY and CUSTOM_RBXM_REGISTRY[bKey] and equipCustomRbxmItemRef then
                equipCustomRbxmItemRef({name = iName, img = iImg, price = iPrice, cat = cat}, CUSTOM_RBXM_REGISTRY[bKey], iName, bKey)
            else
                local clone = deepClone(bkp)
                if clone then
                    bakeItemToAvatar(clone, cat, slotKey, {name = iName, img = iImg, price = iPrice, cat = cat})
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)
-- ============================================================
-- 3. ITEM CATALOG WITH DYNAMIC SERVER SYNCHRONIZATION
-- ============================================================
local categories = { Heels={}, Skirts={}, Bodices={}, Accessories={}, Wings={}, Halos={} }
local catOrder   = {"Worn", "Heels", "Skirts", "Bodices", "Accessories", "Wings", "Halos", "Stats"}
local catalogItemByKey = {}

local tabButtons = {}
local refreshGridRef = nil
local rhImageLookup = nil

local function updateTabCounts()
    for cat, btn in pairs(tabButtons) do
        if cat == "Worn" then
            local okW, wl = pcall(getWornItemsList)
            local count = (okW and type(wl) == "table") and #wl or 0
            btn.Text = "Worn\n(" .. count .. ")"
        elseif cat == "Stats" then
            btn.Text = "Stats\n(Spoof)"
        else
            local count = #(categories[cat] or {})
            btn.Text = cat .. "\n(" .. count .. ")"
        end
    end
end

local function addOrUpdateCatalogItem(cat, alias, assetName, price, img, isServerData)
    if not alias and not assetName then return end
    local rawKey = tostring(assetName or alias)
    local key = rawKey:lower()
    local existing = catalogItemByKey[key]

    local pStr = "N/A"
    if type(price) == "number" then
        pStr = string.format("%d", price):reverse():gsub("(%d%d%d)", "%1,"):reverse():gsub("^,", "")
    elseif type(price) == "string" and price ~= "" then
        pStr = price
    end

    local imgStr = ""
    if type(img) == "string" and img ~= "" then
        if img:find("rbxassetid://") or img:find("http") then
            imgStr = img
        elseif tonumber(img) then
            imgStr = "rbxassetid://" .. img
        end
    elseif type(img) == "number" and img > 0 then
        imgStr = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(img)
    end
    if imgStr == "" and rhImageLookup then
        local c1 = (alias or ""):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
        local c2 = (assetName or ""):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
        local rawImg = rhImageLookup[c1] or rhImageLookup[c2] or ""
        if rawImg ~= "" then
            imgStr = rawImg:find("://", 1, true) and rawImg or ("rbxassetid://" .. rawImg)
        end
    end

    if existing then
        if isServerData and imgStr ~= "" then
            existing.img = imgStr
            existing.isServerImg = true
        elseif (not existing.img or existing.img == "") and imgStr ~= "" then
            existing.img = imgStr
        end
        if pStr ~= "N/A" and (existing.price == "N/A" or isServerData) then
            existing.price = pStr
        end
        return
    end

    local itemObj = {
        name        = alias or assetName,
        assetName   = assetName or alias,
        price       = pStr,
        img         = imgStr,
        cat         = cat,
        isServerImg = (isServerData and imgStr ~= "")
    }
    catalogItemByKey[key] = itemObj
    if alias then
        catalogItemByKey[alias:lower()] = itemObj
        catalogItemByKey[alias:lower():gsub("%s+", "")] = itemObj
    end
    if assetName then
        catalogItemByKey[assetName:lower()] = itemObj
        catalogItemByKey[assetName:lower():gsub("%s+", "")] = itemObj
    end
    if categories[cat] then
        table.insert(categories[cat], itemObj)
    end
end

-- Fast smart catalog thumbnail lookup
findItemImage = function(name, cat)
    if not name or name == "" then return "" end
    local clean = name:lower():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")

    local function expandRbxImg(v)
        if not v or v == "" then return "" end
        return v:find("://", 1, true) and v or ("rbxassetid://" .. v)
    end

    -- 0. Direct lookup from extracted 1,346-item Royale High thumbnail map
    if rhImageLookup[clean] then
        return expandRbxImg(rhImageLookup[clean])
    end

    -- 1. Direct key
    local direct = catalogItemByKey[clean] or catalogItemByKey[name:lower()]
    if direct and direct.img and direct.img ~= "" then
        return direct.img
    end

    -- 2. Scan category list if provided
    local targetList = cat and categories[cat]
    if targetList then
        for _, it in ipairs(targetList) do
            local itClean = it.name:lower():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
            local itAssetClean = (it.assetName or ""):lower():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
            if itClean == clean or itAssetClean == clean or clean:find(itClean, 1, true) or itClean:find(clean, 1, true) then
                if it.img and it.img ~= "" then return it.img end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 3. Scan all catalog items
    for _, it in pairs(catalogItemByKey) do
        local itClean = it.name:lower():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
        local itAssetClean = (it.assetName or ""):lower():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
        if itClean == clean or itAssetClean == clean or clean:find(itClean, 1, true) or itClean:find(clean, 1, true) then
            if it.img and it.img ~= "" then return it.img end
        end
    end

    -- 4. Substring fallback on rhImageLookup
    for k, img in pairs(rhImageLookup) do
        if #k >= 6 and (clean:find(k, 1, true) or k:find(clean, 1, true)) then
            return expandRbxImg(img)
        end
    end

    return ""
end

-- Live Server Synchronizer
local function syncWithLiveServer()
    local net = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Network")
    local fn  = net and net:FindFirstChild("Functions") and net.Functions:FindFirstChild("Gui")
    if not fn then return end

    -- 1. Heels
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            if fn:FindFirstChild("HeelMorphs") and fn.HeelMorphs:FindFirstChild("List") then
                local heels = fn.HeelMorphs.List:InvokeServer()
                if type(heels) == "table" then
                    for assetName, data in pairs(heels) do
                        pcall(function()
                            if type(data) == "table" then
                                local aStr = tostring(assetName)
                                local alias = tostring(data.Alias or aStr)
                                local img = data.Icon or (type(data.Icons) == "table" and data.Icons[1]) or ""
                                addOrUpdateCatalogItem("Heels", alias, aStr, data.Price, img, true)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    pcall(updateTabCounts)
                    if refreshGridRef then refreshGridRef("Heels") end
                end
            end
        end)
    end)

    -- 2. Skirts
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            if fn:FindFirstChild("Skirts") and fn.Skirts:FindFirstChild("List") then
                local skirts = fn.Skirts.List:InvokeServer()
                if type(skirts) == "table" then
                    for assetName, data in pairs(skirts) do
                        pcall(function()
                            if type(data) == "table" then
                                local aStr = tostring(assetName)
                                local alias = tostring(data.Alias or aStr)
                                local img = data.Icon or (type(data.Icons) == "table" and data.Icons[1]) or ""
                                addOrUpdateCatalogItem("Skirts", alias, aStr, data.Price, img, true)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    pcall(updateTabCounts)
                    if refreshGridRef then refreshGridRef("Skirts") end
                end
            end
        end)
    end)

    -- 3. Accessories, Bodices, & Halos
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            if fn:FindFirstChild("Accessories") and fn.Accessories:FindFirstChild("List") then
                local accs = fn.Accessories.List:InvokeServer()
                if type(accs) == "table" then
                    for assetName, data in pairs(accs) do
                        pcall(function()
                            if type(data) == "table" then
                                local aStr = tostring(assetName)
                                local alias = tostring(data.Alias or aStr)
                                local img = data.Icon or (type(data.Icons) == "table" and data.Icons[1]) or ""
                                local sub = tostring(data.Type or data.Category or ""):lower()
                                local aliasL = alias:lower()
                                local assetL = aStr:lower()

                                if aliasL:find("halo") or assetL:find("halo") then
                                    addOrUpdateCatalogItem("Halos", alias, aStr, data.Price or "Special", img, true)
                                elseif aliasL:find("bodice") or aliasL:find("corset") or aliasL:find("jacket") or sub:find("bodice") or sub:find("torso") then
                                    addOrUpdateCatalogItem("Bodices", alias, aStr, data.Price, img, true)
                                else
                                    addOrUpdateCatalogItem("Accessories", alias, aStr, data.Price, img, true)
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    pcall(updateTabCounts)
                    if refreshGridRef then
                        refreshGridRef("Accessories")
                        refreshGridRef("Bodices")
                        refreshGridRef("Halos")
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end)

    -- 4. Wings
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            if fn:FindFirstChild("Wings") and fn.Wings:FindFirstChild("List") then
                local wings = fn.Wings.List:InvokeServer()
                if type(wings) == "table" then
                    for assetName, data in pairs(wings) do
                        pcall(function()
                            if type(data) == "table" then
                                local aStr = tostring(assetName)
                                local alias = tostring(data.Alias or aStr)
                                local img = data.Icon or (type(data.Icons) == "table" and data.Icons[1]) or ""
                                addOrUpdateCatalogItem("Wings", alias, aStr, data.Price, img, true)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    pcall(updateTabCounts)
                    if refreshGridRef then refreshGridRef("Wings") end
                end
            end
        end)
    end)

    task.delay(2.5, function() pcall(updateTabCounts) end)
    task.delay(6.0, function() pcall(updateTabCounts) end)
end

task.spawn(syncWithLiveServer)

-- ============================================================
-- 4. EQUIP & AUTO-STACKING ENGINE
-- ============================================================
local function findShopTryonButton(itemName)
    local shop = pg:FindFirstChild("Shop")
    if not shop then return nil end
    local dir = shop:FindFirstChild("Directory")
    if not dir then return nil end

    local targetLower = itemName:lower()
    for _, folder in ipairs(dir:GetChildren()) do
        local mid = folder:FindFirstChild("MiddlePanel")
        local sf = mid and mid:FindFirstChild("ScrollingFrame")
        if sf then
            for _, itemFrame in ipairs(sf:GetChildren()) do
                if itemFrame:IsA("Frame") then
                    local tryon = itemFrame:FindFirstChild("Tryon")
                    if tryon and tryon.Visible then
                        local fName = itemFrame.Name:lower()
                        local nLabel = itemFrame:FindFirstChild("name")
                        local lText = (nLabel and nLabel:IsA("TextLabel")) and nLabel.Text:lower() or ""
                        if fName == targetLower or lText == targetLower
                           or fName:find(targetLower, 1, true) or targetLower:find(fName, 1, true)
                           or (lText ~= "" and lText:find(targetLower, 1, true)) then
                            return tryon
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function clickButtonVIM(btn)
    if not btn then return false end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local pos = btn.AbsolutePosition
        local sz  = btn.AbsoluteSize
        local cx  = pos.X + sz.X / 2
        local cy  = pos.Y + sz.Y / 2
        if VIM then
            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(cx, cy, 0, true, game, 1)
            task.wait(0.04)
            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(cx, cy, 0, false, game, 1)
        elseif VU then
            VU:Button1Down(Vector2.new(cx, cy), workspace.CurrentCamera)
            task.wait(0.04)
            VU:Button1Up(Vector2.new(cx, cy), workspace.CurrentCamera)
        end
    end)
    return ok
end

local function applyDirectColorToItem(item, c3, group, texOverride)
    if not item or not c3 then return false end
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return false end
    group = tonumber(group) or 1
    local assetName = item.assetName or item.name

    local slotKey = item.slotKey
    if not slotKey or slotKey == "" then
        local cleanKeyName = tostring(item.name or assetName or ""):gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
        if item.cat == "Heels" or item.cat == "Skirts" or item.cat == "Bodices" or item.cat == "Wings" then
            slotKey = item.cat
        elseif item.cat == "Halos" then
            slotKey = "Halo_" .. cleanKeyName
        else
            slotKey = "Acc_" .. cleanKeyName
        end
    end

    local didColor = false
    for _, folder in ipairs({getPreviewFolder(false), getBakedFolder(false)}) do
        if folder then
            local bakedModel = folder:FindFirstChild(slotKey)
            if not bakedModel then
                local cleanA = (item.name or ""):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
                local cleanB = (assetName or ""):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
                for _, ch in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    local cn = (ch:GetAttribute("RH_ItemName") or ch.Name):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
                    if (cleanA ~= "" and (cn == cleanA or cn:find(cleanA, 1, true)))
                       or (cleanB ~= "" and (cn == cleanB or cn:find(cleanB, 1, true))) then
                        bakedModel = ch
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            if bakedModel then
                if RHRigColor.applyColorToModel(bakedModel, char, c3, group, texOverride) then didColor = true end
                if bakedModel:GetAttribute("RH_BakedLayer") then
                    bakedLayersBackup[bakedModel.Name] = deepClone(bakedModel)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    for _, info in ipairs(getEquippedFolders()) do
        for _, child in ipairs(info.folder:GetChildren()) do
            if child.Name == item.name or child.Name == assetName or (not previewBeforeSet[child] and activePreviewItem) then
                if RHRigColor.applyColorToModel(child, char, c3, group, texOverride) then didColor = true end
            end
        end
    end

    return didColor
end

local function openItemColorAndToggles(item)
    if not item then return false end
    local assetName = item.assetName or item.name
    local opened = false

    -- 1. Open Royale High Toggles UI (prefer HUD.PreviewTogglesFrame which is actually visible on-screen!)
    pcall(function()
        local hud = pg:FindFirstChild("HUD")
        local cc = pg:FindFirstChild("CharacterCreation")
        local shop = pg:FindFirstChild("Shop")
        local togglesMod = (cc and cc:FindFirstChild("TogglesLocalModule", true))
                        or (hud and hud:FindFirstChild("TogglesLocalModule", true))
                        or (shop and shop:FindFirstChild("TogglesLocalModule", true))

        local ptf = (hud and hud:FindFirstChild("PreviewTogglesFrame", true))
                 or (shop and shop:FindFirstChild("PreviewTogglesFrame", true))
                 or (cc and cc:FindFirstChild("PreviewTogglesFrame", true))

        if togglesMod and ptf then
            local mod = require(togglesMod)
            if mod and mod.HasToggles and mod:HasToggles(assetName) then
                local sg = ptf:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScreenGui")
                if sg then sg.Enabled = true end
                keepTogglesOpenUntil = tick() + 60
                if not ptf:GetAttribute("RH_PtfGuarded") then
                    ptf:SetAttribute("RH_PtfGuarded", true)
                    ptf:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
                        if not ptf.Visible and tick() < keepTogglesOpenUntil then
                            task.defer(function()
                                if tick() < keepTogglesOpenUntil then ptf.Visible = true end
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                    local closePtfBtn = ptf:FindFirstChild("Close", true) or ptf:FindFirstChild("X", true) or ptf:FindFirstChild("Exit", true)
                    if closePtfBtn and closePtfBtn:IsA("GuiButton") then
                        closePtfBtn.Activated:Connect(function() keepTogglesOpenUntil = 0 end)
                    end
                end
                ptf.Visible = true
                mod:OpenToggleOptions(assetName, ptf)
                opened = true

                -- Preview mode: let user freely switch toggles BEFORE cloning; Baked mode: re-bake updated toggle!
                task.delay(0.18, function()
                    local inner = ptf:FindFirstChild("Inner")
                    if inner then
                        for _, btn in ipairs(inner:GetChildren()) do
                            if btn:IsA("GuiButton") and not btn:GetAttribute("RH_ToggleHooked") then
                                btn:SetAttribute("RH_ToggleHooked", true)
                                btn.Activated:Connect(function()
                                    local tNum = btn:GetAttribute("ToggleNumber")
                                    local toggleAsset = btn:GetAttribute("ActualToggleAsset") or assetName
                                    keepTogglesOpenUntil = tick() + 60

                                    -- Check if this item is currently only being previewed (not yet cloned to bakedFolder)
                                    local bfNow = getBakedFolder(false)
                                    local cleanK = tostring(item.name or assetName or ""):gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
                                    local skCheck = item.slotKey or ((item.cat == "Halos" and "Halo_" or ((item.cat == "Heels" or item.cat == "Skirts" or item.cat == "Bodices" or item.cat == "Wings") and "" or "Acc_")) .. ((item.cat == "Heels" or item.cat == "Skirts" or item.cat == "Bodices" or item.cat == "Wings") and item.cat or cleanK))
                                    local isAlreadyCloned = bfNow and (bfNow:FindFirstChild(skCheck) ~= nil)

                                    if not isAlreadyCloned then
                                        if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("Toggle set! Tap CLONE LOOK when ready", C.text) end
                                        return
                                    end

                                    task.spawn(function()
                                        if not findSpecificItemOnAvatar(toggleAsset, item.cat, nil) then
                                            if previewItemRemote then
                                                pcall(function() previewItemRemote:FireServer(toggleAsset) end)
                                                task.wait(0.35)
                                            end
                                            if tNum then
                                                pcall(function()
                                                    local tn = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("TogglesNetwork")
                                                    local eq = tn and tn:FindFirstChild("Equip")
                                                    if eq then eq:FireServer(assetName, tonumber(tNum), btn:GetAttribute("ActualToggleAsset")) end
                                                end)
                                            end
                                        end
                                        local tStart = tick()
                                        local rebaked = nil
                                        while tick() - tStart < 3.2 do
                                            task.wait(0.15)
                                            local targetInst = findSpecificItemOnAvatar(toggleAsset, item.cat, nil)
                                                            or findSpecificItemOnAvatar(assetName, item.cat, nil)
                                            if targetInst and countBaseParts(targetInst) > 0 then
                                                waitForItemReady(targetInst, lp.Character, 1.5)
                                                rebaked = bakeItemToAvatar(targetInst, item.cat, item.slotKey, item)
                                                if rebaked then break end
                                            end
                                        end
                                        if not rebaked and tNum then
                                            pcall(function()
                                                local inv = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Inventory")
                                                local gd = inv and inv:FindFirstChild("GetDetailedAsset")
                                                if gd and gd:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                                                    local det = gd:InvokeServer(toggleAsset, nil, true)
                                                    if det and type(det.ItemModels) == "table" then
                                                        local mdl = det.ItemModels[tonumber(tNum)] or det.ItemModels[tostring(tNum)]
                                                        if typeof(mdl) == "Instance" then
                                                            local pick = mdl:IsA("Model") and mdl or (mdl:FindFirstChild("Girl") or mdl:FindFirstChild("Woman") or mdl:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Model") or mdl)
                                                            if pick and countBaseParts(pick) > 0 then
                                                                rebaked = bakeItemToAvatar(pick, item.cat, item.slotKey, item)
                                                            end
                                                        end
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end)
                                        end
                                        if rebaked then
                                            local sk = rebaked:GetAttribute("RH_SlotKey") or rebaked.Name
                                            local sc = savedColorsBySlot[sk]
                                            if sc then
                                                for gIdx, cEntry in pairs(sc) do
                                                    applyDirectColorToItem({name=item.name, assetName=assetName, cat=item.cat, slotKey=sk}, cEntry.c3, gIdx, cEntry.tex)
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                        releasePlayerMovement()
                                        updateTabCounts()
                                        if refreshGridRef then refreshGridRef() end
                                        if rebaked and notifyToastRef then
                                            notifyToastRef("TOGGLE CLONED: " .. item.name, 2.0)
                                        end
                                    end)
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    -- 2. Open Royale High official ColorsGui with 2-color palette, SurfaceAppearance support, & strict skin protection
    pcall(function()
        local cg = pg:FindFirstChild("ColorsGui")
        if not cg then return end
        cg.DisplayOrder = 999
        cg.Enabled = true

        local co = cg:FindFirstChild("ColorOptions")
        if not co then return end
        co.Visible = true

        local colorMod = require(co:WaitForChild("ColorOptionsModule"))
        if colorMod and colorMod.Appear then
            local function onColorPicked(chosenColor, colorGroupIndex, multiOptions)
                local c3 = nil
                local texOverride = nil
                if typeof(chosenColor) == "Color3" then
                    c3 = chosenColor
                elseif typeof(chosenColor) == "table" then
                    if chosenColor.r and chosenColor.g and chosenColor.b then
                        c3 = Color3.new(chosenColor.r, chosenColor.g, chosenColor.b)
                    elseif chosenColor[1] and chosenColor[2] and chosenColor[3] then
                        c3 = Color3.new(chosenColor[1], chosenColor[2], chosenColor[3])
                    end
                elseif type(chosenColor) == "string" then
                    if chosenColor:find("rbx") or tonumber(chosenColor) then
                        texOverride = chosenColor:find("rbx") and chosenColor or ("rbxassetid://" .. chosenColor)
                        c3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                    else
                        pcall(function()
                            local gd = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GetDesignColorByName")
                            if gd and gd:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                                local d = gd:InvokeServer(chosenColor)
                                if d and d.Id then
                                    texOverride = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(d.Id)
                                    c3 = (d.Colors and d.Colors[1]) or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end

                if not c3 then return end
                local group = tonumber(tostring(colorGroupIndex or ""):match("%d+")) or 1

                local char = lp.Character
                if not char then return end

                local slotKey = item.slotKey
                if not slotKey or slotKey == "" then
                    local cleanKeyName = tostring(item.name or item.assetName or ""):gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
                    if item.cat == "Heels" or item.cat == "Skirts" or item.cat == "Bodices" or item.cat == "Wings" then
                        slotKey = item.cat
                    elseif item.cat == "Halos" then
                        slotKey = "Halo_" .. cleanKeyName
                    else
                        slotKey = "Acc_" .. cleanKeyName
                    end
                end

                local function applyColorToModelParts(m)
                    return RHRigColor.applyColorToModel(m, char, c3, group, texOverride)
                end

                -- Recolor target item in Preview Stage AND/OR Baked Closet, saving colors for promotion/restore!
                for _, folder in ipairs({getPreviewFolder(false), getBakedFolder(false)}) do
                    if folder then
                        local bakedModel = folder:FindFirstChild(slotKey)
                        if not bakedModel then
                            local cleanA = (item.name or ""):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
                            local cleanB = (assetName or ""):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
                            for _, ch in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                                local cn = (ch:GetAttribute("RH_ItemName") or ch.Name):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
                                if (cleanA ~= "" and (cn == cleanA or cn:find(cleanA, 1, true)))
                                   or (cleanB ~= "" and (cn == cleanB or cn:find(cleanB, 1, true))) then
                                    bakedModel = ch
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if bakedModel then
                            applyColorToModelParts(bakedModel)
                            savedColorsBySlot[bakedModel.Name] = savedColorsBySlot[bakedModel.Name] or {}
                            savedColorsBySlot[bakedModel.Name][group] = { c3 = c3, tex = texOverride }
                            if bakedModel:GetAttribute("RH_BakedLayer") then
                                bakedLayersBackup[bakedModel.Name] = deepClone(bakedModel)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                -- Also save under slotKey directly in case user is coloring a live RH server preview before baking
                savedColorsBySlot[slotKey] = savedColorsBySlot[slotKey] or {}
                savedColorsBySlot[slotKey][group] = { c3 = c3, tex = texOverride }

                -- Send official multi-color update if previewing live server item & multiOptions table provided
                if type(multiOptions) == "table" and activePreviewItem then
                    pcall(function()
                        local tn = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("TogglesNetwork")
                        local eq = tn and tn:FindFirstChild("Equip")
                        if eq then eq:FireServer(assetName, 1, nil, multiOptions, true) end
                    end)
                end

                -- Also recolor matching live try-on item in EquippedStorage / Character if present (including toggled variants!)
                for _, info in ipairs(getEquippedFolders()) do
                    for _, child in ipairs(info.folder:GetChildren()) do
                        if child.Name == item.name or child.Name == assetName or (not previewBeforeSet[child] and activePreviewItem) then
                            applyColorToModelParts(child)
                        end
                    end
                end
                if activePreviewItem then
                    for _, fi in ipairs(findRoyaleHighItemsOnAvatar()) do
                        if not previewBeforeSet[fi] then
                            applyColorToModelParts(fi)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local pf = getPreviewFolder(false)
            local bf = getBakedFolder(false)
            local customMeta, canonCustom = nil, nil
            if findCustomRbxmSpecRef then customMeta, canonCustom = findCustomRbxmSpecRef(item) end

            local function findTargetColorModel()
                local cleanA = tostring(item.name or ""):lower():gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
                local cleanB = tostring(assetName or ""):lower():gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
                for _, folder in ipairs({pf, bf}) do
                    if folder then
                        if item.slotKey and folder:FindFirstChild(item.slotKey) then
                            return folder:FindFirstChild(item.slotKey)
                        end
                        for _, ch in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                            local cn = tostring(ch:GetAttribute("RH_ItemName") or ch.Name):lower():gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
                            if (cleanA ~= "" and cn == cleanA) or (cleanB ~= "" and cn == cleanB) then
                                return ch
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                for _, info in ipairs(getEquippedFolders()) do
                    for _, ch in ipairs(info.folder:GetChildren()) do
                        if ch.Name == item.name or ch.Name == assetName or (not previewBeforeSet[ch] and activePreviewItem) then
                            return ch
                        end
                    end
                end
                return findSpecificItemOnAvatar(assetName, item.cat, nil)
            end

            local bm = findTargetColorModel()
            local modelGroups = bm and RHRigColor.countModelGroups(bm) or 0
            local assetMeta = (not customMeta) and RHRigColor.getAssetColorMeta(assetName) or nil
            local numGroups = 2
            if customMeta then
                numGroups = math.max(customMeta.maxColors or 2, modelGroups)
            else
                local remoteNum = assetMeta and assetMeta.numGroups or 0
                numGroups = math.max(remoteNum, modelGroups)
                if numGroups == 0 then numGroups = 2 end
                if bm then
                    bm:SetAttribute("RH_MaxColorGroup", numGroups)
                end
            end
            numGroups = math.clamp(numGroups, 1, 12)

            colorMod:SetItemToUnequip(assetName, item.cat)
            pcall(function() colorMod:RememberSelectionKey(assetName) end)
            colorMod:Appear(onColorPicked, true, false, "Generic", numGroups, false)
            local names = {}
            local realNames = assetMeta and assetMeta.colorNames or nil
            for idx = 1, numGroups do
                local rn = realNames and realNames[idx]
                names[idx] = (type(rn) == "string" and rn ~= "") and rn or ("Color " .. tostring(idx))
            end
            pcall(function() colorMod:SetNames(unpack(names)) end)
            opened = true
        end
    end)

    return opened
end
