    ["spring22"]     = { name = "Spring Halo 2022", cat = "Halos", maxColors = 4, aliases = {"springhalo2022", "spring22halo"} },
    ["glimmering"]   = { name = "Glimmering Light Halo", cat = "Halos", maxColors = 2, aliases = {"glimmeringlighthalo"} },
    ["alien"]        = { name = "Glimmering Alien Halo", cat = "Halos", maxColors = 2, aliases = {"glimmeringalienhalo", "alienhalo"} },
    ["everfriend24"] = { name = "Everfriend Halo 2024", cat = "Halos", maxColors = 6, aliases = {"everfriendhalo2024", "everfriend24halo"} },
    ["everfriend25"] = { name = "Everfriend Halo 2025", cat = "Halos", maxColors = 10, aliases = {"everfriendhalo2025", "everfriend25halo"} },
    ["everfriend26"] = { name = "Everfriend Halo 2026", cat = "Halos", maxColors = 6, aliases = {"everfriendhalo2026", "everfriend26halo"} },
}

local function b64decode(data)
    local b = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/'
    data = string.gsub(data, '[^'..b..'=]', '')
    return (data:gsub('.', function(x)
        if x == '=' then return '' end
        local r, f = '', (b:find(x) - 1)
        for i = 6, 1, -1 do r = r .. (f % 2^i - f % 2^(i-1) > 0 and '1' or '0') end
        return r
    end):gsub('%d%d%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?', function(x)
        if #x ~= 8 then return '' end
        local c = 0
        for i = 1, 8 do c = c + (x:sub(i,i) == '1' and 2^(8-i) or 0) end
        return string.char(c)
    end))
end

local function loadExactRbxmModel(b64Key)
    local b64 = CUSTOM_RBXM_B64[b64Key]
    if not b64 then return nil end
    local assetFn = getcustomasset or getsynasset
    if not writefile or not assetFn or not game.GetObjects then return nil end

    local ok, model = pcall(function()
        local fname = "RH_Exact_" .. b64Key .. ".rbxm"
        writefile(fname, b64decode(b64))
        local objs = game:GetObjects(assetFn(fname))
        if objs and objs[1] then
            return objs[1]:Clone()
        end
        return nil
    end)
    if ok and model then return model end
    return nil
end

local function findCustomRbxmSpec(item)
    if not item then return nil, nil, nil end
    for _, cand in ipairs({item.assetName, item.name}) do
        if type(cand) == "string" and cand ~= "" then
            local kNoParen = cand:lower():gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
            local kFull    = cand:lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
            for bkey, meta in pairs(CUSTOM_RBXM_REGISTRY) do
                for _, al in ipairs(meta.aliases) do
                    if kNoParen == al or kFull == al then
                        return meta, meta.name, bkey
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil, nil, nil
end
findCustomRbxmSpecRef = findCustomRbxmSpec

local function equipCustomRbxmItem(item, meta, canonName, b64Key, isPreviewOnly)
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char or not meta then return false end

    local cat = item.cat or meta.cat or "Accessories"
    local dispName = canonName or meta.name or item.name
    local cleanCanon = tostring(dispName):gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
    local slotKey = ((cat == "Halos") and "Halo_" or "Acc_") .. cleanCanon

    if isPreviewOnly then
        clearPreviewStage()
    end

    local bakedFolder = isPreviewOnly and getPreviewFolder(true) or getBakedFolder(true)
    local animKey = (isPreviewOnly and "Preview_" or "") .. slotKey
    local hideKey = (isPreviewOnly and "Preview_" or "") .. slotKey

    local old = bakedFolder:FindFirstChild(slotKey)
    if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end
    if (not isPreviewOnly) and cat == "Halos" then
        local legacyH = bakedFolder:FindFirstChild("Halos")
        if legacyH and legacyH:GetAttribute("RH_CustomB64Key") == b64Key then
            pcall(function() legacyH:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    if activeHaloBobConns[animKey] then
        pcall(function() activeHaloBobConns[animKey]:Disconnect() end)
        activeHaloBobConns[animKey] = nil
    end

    local exactModel = b64Key and loadExactRbxmModel(b64Key) or nil
    if not exactModel then
        if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("Missing GetObjects support for " .. dispName, 2.2) end
        return false
    end

    local imgToSave = (item.img and item.img ~= "") and item.img or (findItemImage and findItemImage(dispName, cat) or "")
    exactModel.Name = slotKey
    if isPreviewOnly then
        exactModel:SetAttribute("RH_PreviewItem", true)
    else
        exactModel:SetAttribute("RH_BakedLayer", true)
    end
    exactModel:SetAttribute("RH_Category", cat)
    exactModel:SetAttribute("RH_SlotKey", slotKey)
    exactModel:SetAttribute("RH_ItemName", dispName)
    exactModel:SetAttribute("RH_Image", imgToSave)
    exactModel:SetAttribute("RH_Price", item.price or "Special")
    exactModel:SetAttribute("RH_CustomRbxm", true)
    exactModel:SetAttribute("RH_CustomB64Key", b64Key)

    local anchorWelds = {}
    local mermaidWelds = {}
    local allParts = {}

    -- 1. Strip Scripts/dangling nil joints, set massless/non-colliding, preserve Bones, Motor6Ds, SurfaceAppearances, Particles, Lights!
    for _, d in ipairs(exactModel:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("Script") or d:IsA("LocalScript") then
            pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
        elseif d:IsA("BasePart") then
            d.Anchored = false
            d.CanCollide = false
            d.CanTouch = false
            d.CanQuery = false
            d.Massless = true
            if HELPER_TRANSPARENT_PARTS[d.Name] then
                d.Transparency = 1
            end
            d:SetAttribute("RH_OrigTransparency", d.Transparency)
            table.insert(allParts, d)
        elseif (d:IsA("Weld") or d:IsA("WeldConstraint")) and d.Name == "AnchorWeld" then
            table.insert(anchorWelds, d)
        elseif (d:IsA("Weld") or d:IsA("WeldConstraint")) and d.Name == "MermaidBodyPartWeld" then
            table.insert(mermaidWelds, d)
        elseif d:IsA("WeldConstraint") and (not d.Part0 or not d.Part1) then
            pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
        end
    end

    local function makeWeld(p0, p1, wName)
        local w = Instance.new("Weld")
        w.Name = wName or "RH_RigidWeld"
        w.Part0 = p0
        w.Part1 = p1
        w.C0 = p0.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(p1.CFrame)
        w.C1 = CFrame.new()
        w.Parent = p1
        return w
    end

    local function convertInternalWeldConstraintsToWelds()
        local existingWeldPair = {}
        for _, j in ipairs(exactModel:GetDescendants()) do
            if j:IsA("Weld") and j.Part0 and j.Part1 and j.Part0 ~= j.Part1 then
                existingWeldPair[j.Part0] = existingWeldPair[j.Part0] or {}
                existingWeldPair[j.Part0][j.Part1] = true
                existingWeldPair[j.Part1] = existingWeldPair[j.Part1] or {}
                existingWeldPair[j.Part1][j.Part0] = true
            end
        end
        for _, j in ipairs(exactModel:GetDescendants()) do
            if j:IsA("WeldConstraint") and j.Name ~= "AnchorWeld" and j.Name ~= "MermaidBodyPartWeld" then
                local p0, p1 = j.Part0, j.Part1
                if p0 and p1 and p0 ~= p1 and p0:IsA("BasePart") and p1:IsA("BasePart")
                   and (p0:IsDescendantOf(exactModel)) and (p1:IsDescendantOf(exactModel)) then
                    if not (existingWeldPair[p0] and existingWeldPair[p0][p1]) then
                        makeWeld(p0, p1, j.Name)
                        existingWeldPair[p0] = existingWeldPair[p0] or {}
                        existingWeldPair[p0][p1] = true
                        existingWeldPair[p1] = existingWeldPair[p1] or {}
                        existingWeldPair[p1][p0] = true
                    end
                end
                pcall(function() j:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
    end

    -- Snapshot original studio CFrames of EVERY part BEFORE moving anything!
    local origCF = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(allParts) do origCF[p] = p.CFrame end

    local headAnchorWeld = nil
    local headAnchorPart = nil
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")

    if #mermaidWelds > 0 or b64Key == "tail" then
        -- =========================================================================
        -- TAIL.RBXM 1:1 ARTICULATED MERMAID SPINE (Centered LowerTorsoCR + Motors + BoneMatcher!)
        -- Keep LeftHip, LeftKnee, LeftAnkle Motor6Ds so LowerTorsoCR -> LeftUpperLegCR -> LeftLowerLegCR -> LeftFootCR
        -- stays centered (X=0) & 1:1 to the file instead of splitting onto char.LeftUpperLeg!
        -- =========================================================================
        local lowerTorsoCR = nil
        local lowerWeld = nil
        for _, mw in ipairs(mermaidWelds) do
            local crPart = (mw.Part1 and mw.Part1:IsA("BasePart") and mw.Part1) or (mw.Parent and mw.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and mw.Parent)
            if crPart and crPart.Name == "LowerTorsoCR" then
                lowerTorsoCR = crPart
                lowerWeld = mw
            else
                pcall(function() mw:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        lowerTorsoCR = lowerTorsoCR or exactModel:FindFirstChild("LowerTorsoCR", true)

        if lowerTorsoCR then
            local cLower = resolveCharLimb(char, "LowerTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or hrp
            local ltOrig = origCF[lowerTorsoCR]
            local relLT = (lowerWeld and lowerWeld:IsA("Weld")) and (lowerWeld.C0 * lowerWeld.C1:Inverse()) or CFrame.new()
            local targetLTCF = (cLower and cLower.CFrame or CFrame.new()) * relLT

            -- Move EVERY part in Tail rigidly as 1:1 assembly before parenting or converting constraints!
            for _, p in ipairs(allParts) do
                p.CFrame = targetLTCF * ltOrig:ToObjectSpace(origCF[p])
            end

            convertInternalWeldConstraintsToWelds()

            -- Check reachability from LowerTorsoCR across all surviving Welds & Motor6Ds
            local adj = {}
            for _, j in ipairs(exactModel:GetDescendants()) do
                if (j:IsA("Weld") or j:IsA("Motor6D")) and j.Part0 and j.Part1 and j.Part0 ~= j.Part1 then
                    adj[j.Part0] = adj[j.Part0] or {}
                    adj[j.Part1] = adj[j.Part1] or {}
                    table.insert(adj[j.Part0], j.Part1)
                    table.insert(adj[j.Part1], j.Part0)
                end
            end
            local vis = { [lowerTorsoCR] = true }
            local q = { lowerTorsoCR }
            local h = 1
            while h <= #q do
                local cur = q[h]; h = h + 1
                if adj[cur] then
                    for _, nb in ipairs(adj[cur]) do
                        if not vis[nb] then vis[nb] = true; table.insert(q, nb) end
                    end
                end
            end
            for _, p in ipairs(allParts) do
                if not vis[p] then
                    makeWeld(lowerTorsoCR, p, "RH_TailOrphanWeld")
                end
            end

            if cLower then
                if not lowerWeld or not lowerWeld:IsA("Weld") then
                    if lowerWeld then pcall(function() lowerWeld:Destroy() end) end
                    lowerWeld = makeWeld(cLower, lowerTorsoCR, "MermaidBodyPartWeld")
                    lowerWeld.C0 = relLT
                else
                    lowerWeld.Part0 = cLower
                    lowerWeld.Part1 = lowerTorsoCR
                end
                lowerWeld:SetAttribute("RH_SavedPart0", cLower.Name)
                lowerWeld:SetAttribute("RH_SavedC0", lowerWeld.C0)
            end
        end
    else
        -- =========================================================================
        -- ALL OTHER 15 RBXMS: 1:1 RIGID ISLAND TRANSFORM + ORPHAN MESH RESCUE!
        -- =========================================================================
        -- On Halos, remove redundant AccessoryPartWeld constraints on parts that already have dedicated motion/base Welds or Motor6Ds
        if cat == "Halos" then
            local hasDedicatedJoint = {}
            for _, j in ipairs(exactModel:GetDescendants()) do
                if (j:IsA("Weld") or j:IsA("Motor6D") or j:IsA("WeldConstraint"))
                   and j.Name ~= "AccessoryPartWeld" and j.Name ~= "AnchorWeld" then
                    if j.Part0 and j.Part1 and j.Part0 ~= j.Part1
                       and j.Part0:IsDescendantOf(exactModel) and j.Part1:IsDescendantOf(exactModel) then
                        hasDedicatedJoint[j.Part0] = true
                        hasDedicatedJoint[j.Part1] = true
                    end
                end
            end
            for _, j in ipairs(exactModel:GetDescendants()) do
                if j:IsA("WeldConstraint") and j.Name == "AccessoryPartWeld" then
                    local p0, p1 = j.Part0, j.Part1
                    if (p0 and hasDedicatedJoint[p0]) or (p1 and hasDedicatedJoint[p1]) then
                        pcall(function() j:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local anchors = {}
        local isAnchorPart = {}
        for _, aw in ipairs(anchorWelds) do
            local mPart = (aw.Part1 and aw.Part1:IsA("BasePart") and aw.Part1) or (aw.Parent and aw.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and aw.Parent)
            if mPart and not isAnchorPart[mPart] then
                local cLimb = resolveCharLimb(char, mPart.Name)
                           or char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                           or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                           or hrp
                if cLimb then
                    isAnchorPart[mPart] = true
                    table.insert(anchors, { aw = aw, mPart = mPart, cLimb = cLimb, parts = {} })
                end
            end
        end

        if #anchors == 0 and #allParts > 0 then
            local mPart = (exactModel:IsA("Model") and exactModel.PrimaryPart)
                       or exactModel:FindFirstChild("Head", true)
                       or exactModel:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso", true)
                       or allParts[1]
            local cLimb = (cat == "Halos" and char:FindFirstChild("Head"))
                       or resolveCharLimb(char, mPart.Name)
                       or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                       or hrp
            if mPart and cLimb then
                isAnchorPart[mPart] = true
                table.insert(anchors, { aw = nil, mPart = mPart, cLimb = cLimb, parts = {} })
            end
        end

        -- Build internal adjacency graph across all surviving internal joints (without bridging two distinct anchor limbs)
        local adj = {}
        for _, j in ipairs(exactModel:GetDescendants()) do
            if (j:IsA("Weld") or j:IsA("WeldConstraint") or j:IsA("Motor6D"))
               and j.Name ~= "AnchorWeld" and j.Part0 and j.Part1 and j.Part0 ~= j.Part1 then
                local a, b = j.Part0, j.Part1
                if a:IsA("BasePart") and b:IsA("BasePart") and a:IsDescendantOf(exactModel) and b:IsDescendantOf(exactModel) then
                    if not (isAnchorPart[a] and isAnchorPart[b]) then
                        adj[a] = adj[a] or {}
                        adj[b] = adj[b] or {}
                        table.insert(adj[a], b)
                        table.insert(adj[b], a)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local assignedAnchor = {}
        for _, aInfo in ipairs(anchors) do
            local q = { aInfo.mPart }
            assignedAnchor[aInfo.mPart] = aInfo
            table.insert(aInfo.parts, aInfo.mPart)
            local h = 1
            while h <= #q do
                local cur = q[h]; h = h + 1
                if adj[cur] then
                    for _, nb in ipairs(adj[cur]) do
                        if not assignedAnchor[nb] and not isAnchorPart[nb] then
                            assignedAnchor[nb] = aInfo
                            table.insert(aInfo.parts, nb)
                            table.insert(q, nb)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- Rescue ANY unreached/orphan meshes (Crown shell pearl, Scepter fin, Spring 2022 NoWeld, Val 2020 snowflakes, etc.)!
        local orphanTargets = {}
        local primaryAnchor = anchors[1]
        if primaryAnchor then
            for _, p in ipairs(allParts) do
                if not assignedAnchor[p] then
                    -- Pick nearest anchor in original studio space
                    local bestAnchor = primaryAnchor
                    local bestDist = math.huge
                    for _, aInfo in ipairs(anchors) do
                        local d = (origCF[p].Position - origCF[aInfo.mPart].Position).Magnitude
                        if d < bestDist then
                            bestDist = d
                            bestAnchor = aInfo
                        end
                    end
                    -- Attach to halo motion base if inside that island (so NoWeld/snowflakes float with the halo!)
                    local attachRoot = bestAnchor.mPart
                    for _, candidateName in ipairs({"__HaloMotionRoot_Everfriend2026Float", "Track", "StationaryHaloBase", "FakeHead"}) do
                        for _, ip in ipairs(bestAnchor.parts) do
                            if ip.Name == candidateName then
                                attachRoot = ip
                                break
                            end
                        end
                        if attachRoot ~= bestAnchor.mPart then break end
                    end
                    assignedAnchor[p] = bestAnchor
                    table.insert(bestAnchor.parts, p)
                    orphanTargets[p] = attachRoot
                end
            end
        end

        -- Rigidly move EVERY island 1:1 from studio space to target char limb BEFORE creating Welds or parenting!
        for _, aInfo in ipairs(anchors) do
            local mPart = aInfo.mPart
            local cLimb = aInfo.cLimb
            local aw = aInfo.aw
            local mOrig = origCF[mPart]
            local relAnchor = (aw and aw:IsA("Weld")) and (aw.C0 * aw.C1:Inverse()) or CFrame.new()
            local targetMCF = cLimb.CFrame * relAnchor
            for _, p in ipairs(aInfo.parts) do
                p.CFrame = targetMCF * mOrig:ToObjectSpace(origCF[p])
            end
        end

        -- Convert all internal WeldConstraints into explicit Welds & weld rescued orphans 1:1!
        convertInternalWeldConstraintsToWelds()
        for p, attachRoot in pairs(orphanTargets) do
            makeWeld(attachRoot, p, "RH_OrphanRescueWeld")
        end

        -- Bind AnchorWelds cleanly onto avatar limbs
        for _, aInfo in ipairs(anchors) do
            local mPart, cLimb, aw = aInfo.mPart, aInfo.cLimb, aInfo.aw
            if not aw or not aw:IsA("Weld") then
                local prevRel = (aw and aw:IsA("Weld")) and (aw.C0 * aw.C1:Inverse()) or CFrame.new()
                if aw then pcall(function() aw:Destroy() end) end
                aw = makeWeld(cLimb, mPart, "AnchorWeld")
                aw.C0 = prevRel
            else
                aw.Part0 = cLimb
                aw.Part1 = mPart
            end
            aw:SetAttribute("RH_SavedPart0", cLimb.Name)
            aw:SetAttribute("RH_SavedC0", aw.C0)
            if mPart.Name == "Head" or mPart.Name == "Middle" then
                headAnchorWeld = aw
                headAnchorPart = mPart
            end
        end
    end

    RHRigColor.formatModel(exactModel, b64Key)
    RHRigColor.applySkinToModel(exactModel, char)

    local hiddenMap = {}
    if meta.hideLegs then
        for _, lName in ipairs(legPartNames) do hiddenMap[lName] = true end
    end
    if meta.hideUpperTorso then
        hiddenMap["UpperTorso"] = true
        hiddenMap["Torso"] = true
    end
    if meta.hideLowerTorso then
        hiddenMap["LowerTorso"] = true
    end
    if meta.hideTorso then
        hiddenMap["UpperTorso"] = true
        hiddenMap["LowerTorso"] = true
        hiddenMap["Torso"] = true
    end
    if meta.hideArms then
        for _, aName in ipairs({"LeftUpperArm","LeftLowerArm","Left Arm","RightUpperArm","RightLowerArm","Right Arm"}) do
            hiddenMap[aName] = true
        end
    end
    if next(hiddenMap) ~= nil then
        hiddenBodyPartsBySlot[hideKey] = hiddenMap
        enforceHiddenBodyParts()
    end

    exactModel.Parent = bakedFolder

    -- Animations: Tail bonematcher + centered swim/walk curl, OR Halo float + CloudMotor + Glow shimmer!
    if b64Key == "tail" or #mermaidWelds > 0 then
        local bonePairs = {}
        local foldersChecked = {}
        for _, b in ipairs(exactModel:GetDescendants()) do
            if b:IsA("Bone") then
                local mp = b:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("MeshPart")
                local parModel = mp and mp.Parent
                if mp and parModel and mp.Name == "1" and not foldersChecked[parModel] then
                    foldersChecked[parModel] = true
                    local srcBones = {}
                    for _, sb in ipairs(mp:GetDescendants()) do
                        if sb:IsA("Bone") then srcBones[sb.Name] = sb end
                    end
                    for _, sib in ipairs(parModel:GetChildren()) do
                        if sib:IsA("MeshPart") and sib ~= mp then
                            for _, tb in ipairs(sib:GetDescendants()) do
                                if tb:IsA("Bone") and srcBones[tb.Name] then
                                    table.insert(bonePairs, { src = srcBones[tb.Name], dst = tb })
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local mHip = exactModel:FindFirstChild("LeftHip", true)
        local mKnee = exactModel:FindFirstChild("LeftKnee", true)
        local mAnkle = exactModel:FindFirstChild("LeftAnkle", true)
        local t0 = tick()
        local connRef = nil
        connRef = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not exactModel.Parent or not lp.Character then
                if connRef then connRef:Disconnect() end
                if activeHaloBobConns[animKey] == connRef then activeHaloBobConns[animKey] = nil end
                if activeHaloBobConns[slotKey] == connRef then activeHaloBobConns[slotKey] = nil end
                return
            end
            local dt = tick() - t0
            local spd = 0
            local rPart = lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if rPart then
                local v = rPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                spd = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z).Magnitude
            end
            local freq = spd > 1.5 and 4.8 or 1.8
            local amp  = spd > 1.5 and 0.14 or 0.045
            local ph   = dt * freq
            if mHip and mHip:IsA("Motor6D") and mHip.Parent then
                mHip.Transform = CFrame.Angles(math.sin(ph) * amp, 0, 0)
            end
            if mKnee and mKnee:IsA("Motor6D") and mKnee.Parent then
                mKnee.Transform = CFrame.Angles(math.sin(ph - 0.9) * (amp * 1.25), 0, 0)
            end
            if mAnkle and mAnkle:IsA("Motor6D") and mAnkle.Parent then
                mAnkle.Transform = CFrame.Angles(math.sin(ph - 1.8) * (amp * 1.4), 0, 0)
            end
            for i = 1, #bonePairs do
                local bp = bonePairs[i]
                if bp.src.Parent and bp.dst.Parent then
                    bp.dst.WorldCFrame = bp.src.WorldCFrame
                end
            end
        end)
        activeHaloBobConns[animKey] = connRef
    elseif cat == "Halos" then
        local cloudMotors = {}
        for _, m in ipairs(exactModel:GetDescendants()) do
            if m:IsA("Motor6D") and m.Name == "CloudMotor" then
                table.insert(cloudMotors, m)
            end
        end
        local fadeParts = {}
        if b64Key == "glimmering" or b64Key == "alien" then
            for _, p in ipairs(exactModel:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("MeshPart") and p.Name == "neon" and (p:GetAttribute("RH_OrigTransparency") or 1) < 0.95 then
                    table.insert(fadeParts, { part = p, baseTr = p:GetAttribute("RH_OrigTransparency") or p.Transparency })
                end
            end
        end
        local bobWeld = headAnchorWeld
        local baseC0 = bobWeld and bobWeld.C0 or CFrame.new()
        local p0IsChar = bobWeld and bobWeld.Part0 and bobWeld.Part0:IsDescendantOf(char)
        local t0 = tick()
        local connRef = nil
        connRef = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not exactModel.Parent or not lp.Character or (bobWeld and not bobWeld.Parent) then
                if connRef then connRef:Disconnect() end
                if activeHaloBobConns[animKey] == connRef then activeHaloBobConns[animKey] = nil end
                if activeHaloBobConns[slotKey] == connRef then activeHaloBobConns[slotKey] = nil end
                return
            end
            local elapsed = tick() - t0
            if bobWeld then
                local dy = math.sin(elapsed * 2.2) * 0.12
                if p0IsChar then
                    bobWeld.C0 = CFrame.new(0, dy, 0) * baseC0
                else
                    bobWeld.C0 = baseC0 * CFrame.new(0, dy, 0)
                end
            end
            for idx, cm in ipairs(cloudMotors) do
                if cm.Parent then
                    cm.Transform = CFrame.new(0, math.sin(elapsed * 1.8 + idx) * 0.08, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, elapsed * 0.45 * (idx % 2 == 0 and 1 or -1), 0)
                end
            end
            for _, fp in ipairs(fadeParts) do
                if fp.part and fp.part.Parent then
                    fp.part.Transparency = math.clamp(fp.baseTr + math.sin(elapsed * 2.6) * 0.12, 0.05, 0.92)
                end
            end
        end)
        activeHaloBobConns[animKey] = connRef
    end

    -- Re-apply any saved colors on this slot
    if savedColorsBySlot[slotKey] then
        for gIdx, cEntry in pairs(savedColorsBySlot[slotKey]) do
            applyDirectColorToItem({name=dispName, assetName=dispName, cat=cat, slotKey=slotKey}, cEntry.c3, gIdx, cEntry.tex)
        end
    end

    if isPreviewOnly then
        activePreviewItem = item
        if updateInspBtnRef then updateInspBtnRef() end
        if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("PREVIEW: Pick toggle/colors, then CLONE LOOK!", 2.6) end
        if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("Preview ready - choose toggles/colors!", C.text) end
    else
        bakedLayersBackup[slotKey] = deepClone(exactModel)
        updateTabCounts()
        if refreshGridRef then refreshGridRef() end
        if updateInspBtnRef then updateInspBtnRef() end
        if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("CLONED: " .. dispName .. "!", 2.2) end
        if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("Cloned: " .. dispName, C.text) end
    end
    return true
end
equipCustomRbxmItemRef = equipCustomRbxmItem

local function equipWings(item, isPreviewOnly)
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return "No character" end
    local itemName = item.assetName or item.name
    if isPreviewOnly then
        clearPreviewStage()
        if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("PREVIEWING " .. item.name .. "...", 1.5) end
    else
        if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("CLONING " .. item.name .. "...", 1.5) end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        disableFreezeOnlyConnections()
        local wingInst = nil

        pcall(function()
            local net = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Network")
            local fn = net and net:FindFirstChild("Functions") and net.Functions:FindFirstChild("Gui")
            local wFolder = fn and fn:FindFirstChild("Wings")
            local tryOnFn = wFolder and wFolder:FindFirstChild("TryOn")
            if tryOnFn and tryOnFn:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                local wModel = tryOnFn:InvokeServer(itemName)
                if typeof(wModel) == "Instance" then
                    wingInst = wModel:FindFirstChild("Chest") or wModel
                end
            end
        end)

        if not wingInst or countBaseParts(wingInst) == 0 then
            task.wait(0.25)
            wingInst = char:FindFirstChild("Wings_TRYON")
            if not wingInst then
                for _, info in ipairs(getEquippedFolders()) do
                    if info.category == "Wings" then
                        local fCh = info.folder:GetChildren()
                        if #fCh > 0 then wingInst = fCh[#fCh] break end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if not wingInst or countBaseParts(wingInst) == 0 then
            pcall(function()
                local getWingFn = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GetWingModel")
                if getWingFn and getWingFn:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                    local wm = getWingFn:InvokeServer(itemName)
                    if typeof(wm) == "Instance" then
                        wingInst = wm:FindFirstChild("Chest") or wm
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        local baked = nil
        if wingInst and countBaseParts(wingInst) > 0 then
            baked = bakeItemToAvatar(wingInst, "Wings", "Wings", item, isPreviewOnly)
        end

        hideTryOnOverlays()
        releasePlayerMovement()
        if isPreviewOnly then
            if baked then
                activePreviewItem = item
                if updateInspBtnRef then updateInspBtnRef() end
                if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("PREVIEW: Pick colors, then CLONE LOOK!", 2.4) end
                if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("Preview ready - tap CLONE LOOK!", C.text) end
                task.defer(function() openItemColorAndToggles(item) end)
            else
                if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("Wings failed to preview", 2.0) end
                if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("Wings Preview Failed", C.muted) end
            end
        else
            updateTabCounts()
            if refreshGridRef then refreshGridRef() end
            if baked then
                if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("CLONED: " .. item.name .. "!", 2.2) end
                if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("Wings Equipped!", C.text) end
            else
                if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("Wings failed to load", 2.0) end
                if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("Wings Failed", C.muted) end
            end
        end
    end)

    return "OK"
end

-- Helper: Trigger Shop TryOn invisibly via firesignal + VIM/VU fallback
local function triggerShopTryOnAnyWay(dispName, assetName)
    local btn = findShopTryonButton(dispName) or findShopTryonButton(assetName)
    if not btn then return false end
    local clicked = false
    if firesignal then
        pcall(function() firesignal(btn.MouseButton1Click) clicked = true end)
        pcall(function() firesignal(btn.Activated) clicked = true end)
    end
    if not clicked or btn.Visible then
        task.spawn(function() clickButtonVIM(btn) end)
    end
    return true
end

-- Fallback Client Model Acquisition via Inventory.GetDetailedAsset / TogglesNetwork.GetTogglesInfo
-- Guarantees 1-click cloning even for CantTryOn items or laggy server PreviewItem!
local function tryDirectClientModelFallback(item, itemName, itemCat, previewOnly)
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return nil end

    local function extractBodyModel(mdl)
        if typeof(mdl) ~= "Instance" then return nil end
        if countBaseParts(mdl) > 0 then
            local g = mdl:FindFirstChild("Girl") or mdl:FindFirstChild("Woman") or mdl:FindFirstChild("Boy")
            if g and countBaseParts(g) > 0 then return g end
            return mdl
        end
        for _, ch in ipairs(mdl:GetChildren()) do
            if countBaseParts(ch) > 0 then return ch end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local candidates = {}
    pcall(function()
        local tn = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("TogglesNetwork")
        local gti = tn and tn:FindFirstChild("GetTogglesInfo")
        if gti and gti:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
            local res = gti:InvokeServer(itemName)
            if res and type(res.ItemModels) == "table" then
                for _, m in pairs(res.ItemModels) do
                    local ext = extractBodyModel(m)
                    if ext then table.insert(candidates, ext) break end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    if #candidates == 0 then
        for _, queryName in ipairs({itemName, item.name}) do
            if type(queryName) == "string" and queryName ~= "" then
                pcall(function()
                    local inv = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Inventory")
                    local gd = inv and inv:FindFirstChild("GetDetailedAsset")
                    if gd and gd:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                        local det = gd:InvokeServer(queryName, nil, true)
                        if det and type(det.ItemModels) == "table" then
                            for _, m in pairs(det.ItemModels) do
                                local ext = extractBodyModel(m)
                                if ext then table.insert(candidates, ext) break end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                if #candidates > 0 then break end
            end
        end
    end

    if #candidates == 0 then return nil end
    local raw = candidates[1]:Clone()
    -- If raw has AnchorWelds/helper parts inside, bind AnchorWelds directly onto char before baking!
    for _, d in ipairs(raw:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") and HELPER_TRANSPARENT_PARTS[d.Name] then
            d.Transparency = 1
        elseif (d:IsA("Weld") or d:IsA("WeldConstraint")) and d.Name == "AnchorWeld" then
            local mp = (d.Part1 and d.Part1:IsA("BasePart") and d.Part1) or d.Parent
            if mp and mp:IsA("BasePart") then
                local cl = resolveCharLimb(char, mp.Name) or char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                if cl then
                    local rel = d:IsA("Weld") and (d.C0 * d.C1:Inverse()) or CFrame.new()
                    mp.CFrame = cl.CFrame * rel
                    mp:SetAttribute("RH_LimbHint", cl.Name)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local baked = bakeItemToAvatar(raw, itemCat, nil, item, previewOnly)
    pcall(function() raw:Destroy() end)
    return baked
end

-- Serialized Equip Queue: prevents overlapping rapid taps / ExitPreview races!
local equipQueue = {}
local isEquipWorkerRunning = false

local function executeSingleEquip(item)
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return end
    clearPreviewStage()
    if hideCustomToggleBarRef then hideCustomToggleBarRef() end

    local itemName = item.assetName or item.name
    local itemCat  = item.cat

    -- 1. Check exact local .rbxm registry first (0ms instant load!)
    local customMeta, canonName, b64Key = findCustomRbxmSpec(item)
    if customMeta then
        equipCustomRbxmItem(item, customMeta, canonName, b64Key)
        return
    end

    -- 2. Wings pipeline
    if itemCat == "Wings" then
        equipWings(item)
        return
    end

    if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("CLONING " .. item.name .. "...", 1.8) end

    RHRigColor.snapshotOrigHipHeight(char)

    -- 3. Clean server preview state ONLY if server actually has an active preview (never wipe owned outfit!)
    if exitPreviewRemote and serverPreviewActive then
        pcall(function() exitPreviewRemote:FireServer() end)
        local waitEx = tick()
        while serverPreviewActive and (tick() - waitEx) < 0.75 do
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
        task.wait(0.10)
    end

    -- Snapshot existing items + attach instant ChildAdded catchers across all folders
    local beforeSet = {}
    local instantSpawned = nil
    local childConns = {}

    local function watchContainer(folder, catHint)
        for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            beforeSet[child] = true
        end
        local c = folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            if not isBaseAvatarObject(ch) and not instantSpawned then
                instantSpawned = ch
            end
        end)
        table.insert(childConns, c)
    end

    for _, info in ipairs(getEquippedFolders()) do
        watchContainer(info.folder, info.category)
    end
    watchContainer(char, itemCat)

    disableFreezeOnlyConnections()
    hideTryOnOverlays()

    local fireTick = tick()
    if previewItemRemote then
        pcall(function() previewItemRemote:FireServer(itemName) end)
    else
        triggerShopTryOnAnyWay(item.name, itemName)
    end

    local targetInst, foundCat = nil, nil
    local baked = nil
    local retry1, retry2 = false, false

    local startWait = tick()
    while (tick() - startWait) < 3.6 do
        task.wait(0.08)
        local elapsed = tick() - startWait
        local serverConfirmed = (serverPreviewTick >= fireTick)

        -- Only retry if the server STILL hasn't confirmed PreviewItem & nothing spawned yet
        if elapsed >= 0.55 and not retry1 and not serverConfirmed and not instantSpawned then
            retry1 = true
            if previewItemRemote then
                pcall(function() previewItemRemote:FireServer(itemName) end)
                if item.name ~= itemName then
                    pcall(function() previewItemRemote:FireServer(item.name) end)
                end
            end
            triggerShopTryOnAnyWay(item.name, itemName)
        elseif elapsed >= 1.35 and not retry2 and not serverConfirmed and not instantSpawned then
            retry2 = true
            if previewItemRemote then pcall(function() previewItemRemote:FireServer(itemName) end) end
        end

        if instantSpawned and instantSpawned.Parent and countBaseParts(instantSpawned) > 0 then
            targetInst = instantSpawned
            foundCat = itemCat
            break
        end
        local fInst, fCat = findSpecificItemOnAvatar(itemName, itemCat, beforeSet)
        if fInst then
            targetInst, foundCat = fInst, fCat
            break
        end
    end

    for _, c in ipairs(childConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end

    -- 4. Settle + Weld-Ready Wait -> Precision Multi-Limb & Island Bake!
    if targetInst and waitForItemReady(targetInst, char, 2.2) then
        baked = bakeItemToAvatar(targetInst, itemCat or foundCat, nil, item)
        if baked then
            local initialParts = countBaseParts(baked)
            local trackedSource = targetInst
            task.spawn(function()
                for _, delaySec in ipairs({0.85, 1.4}) do
                    task.wait(delaySec)
                    if trackedSource and trackedSource.Parent then
                        local curParts = countBaseParts(trackedSource)
                        if curParts > initialParts then
                            local updated = bakeItemToAvatar(trackedSource, itemCat or foundCat, nil, item)
                            if updated then
                                initialParts = countBaseParts(updated)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    -- 5. Fallback A: Un-indexed non-base child check
    if not baked then
        for _, fi in ipairs(findRoyaleHighItemsOnAvatar()) do
            if not beforeSet[fi] and countBaseParts(fi) > 0 then
                waitForItemReady(fi, char, 1.4)
                baked = bakeItemToAvatar(fi, itemCat, nil, item)
                if baked then break end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 6. Fallback B: Instant Local Session Cache (0ms guaranteed)
    local cKey = ((itemCat or "") .. "|" .. (itemName or item.name)):lower()
    if not baked and clientItemCache[cKey] then
        local cachedClone = deepClone(clientItemCache[cKey])
        if cachedClone then
            baked = bakeItemToAvatar(cachedClone, itemCat, nil, item)
        end
    end

    -- 7. Fallback C: Direct GetTogglesInfo / GetDetailedAsset ItemModels spawn (bypasses CantTryOn/server TryOn failures!)
    if not baked then
        baked = tryDirectClientModelFallback(item, itemName, itemCat)
    end

    hideTryOnOverlays()
    releasePlayerMovement()
    updateTabCounts()
    if refreshGridRef then refreshGridRef() end
    if baked then
        if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("CLONED: " .. item.name .. "!", 2.2) end
        if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("Cloned & Locked!", C.text) end
    else
        if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("Clone timed out - tap Bake/Lock", 2.0) end
        if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("Try tapping Bake/Lock", C.sub) end
    end
end

local function equipItem(item)
    if not lp.Character or not item then return "No character" end
    -- Deduplicate if identical item is already pending at tail of queue
    for _, qItem in ipairs(equipQueue) do
        if (qItem.assetName or qItem.name) == (item.assetName or item.name) then
            return "OK"
        end
    end
    table.insert(equipQueue, item)
    if not isEquipWorkerRunning then
        isEquipWorkerRunning = true
        task.spawn(function()
            while #equipQueue > 0 do
                local nextItem = table.remove(equipQueue, 1)
                pcall(executeSingleEquip, nextItem)
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
            isEquipWorkerRunning = false
        end)
    end
    return "OK"
end
equipItemRef = equipItem

-- Preview-First Engine: equips/previews item WITHOUT cloning so user can choose toggles & colors FIRST!
startPreviewRef = function(item)
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char or not item then return "No character" end
    local itemName = item.assetName or item.name
    local itemCat  = item.cat

    local customMeta, canonName, b64Key = findCustomRbxmSpec(item)
    if customMeta then
        equipCustomRbxmItem(item, customMeta, canonName, b64Key, true)
        task.defer(function()
            openItemColorAndToggles(item)
            if UI.openCustomToggleBarFor then UI.openCustomToggleBarFor(item) end
        end)
        return "OK"
    end

    if itemCat == "Wings" then
        if hideCustomToggleBarRef then hideCustomToggleBarRef() end
        return equipWings(item, true)
    end

    clearPreviewStage()
    RHRigColor.snapshotOrigHipHeight(char)
    if hideCustomToggleBarRef then hideCustomToggleBarRef() end
    if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("LOADING PREVIEW: " .. item.name .. "...", 1.8) end

    task.spawn(function()
        if exitPreviewRemote and serverPreviewActive then
            pcall(function() exitPreviewRemote:FireServer() end)
            local waitEx = tick()
            while serverPreviewActive and (tick() - waitEx) < 0.75 do
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
            task.wait(0.10)
        end

        previewBeforeSet = {}
        local instantSpawned = nil
        local childConns = {}
        local function watchFolder(folder)
            for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                previewBeforeSet[child] = true
            end
            local c = folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
                if not isBaseAvatarObject(ch) and not instantSpawned then
                    instantSpawned = ch
                end
            end)
            table.insert(childConns, c)
        end
        for _, info in ipairs(getEquippedFolders()) do watchFolder(info.folder) end
        watchFolder(char)

        disableFreezeOnlyConnections()
        hideTryOnOverlays()

        local fireTick = tick()
        if previewItemRemote then
            pcall(function() previewItemRemote:FireServer(itemName) end)
        else
            triggerShopTryOnAnyWay(item.name, itemName)
        end

        local targetInst = nil
        local retry1 = false
        local tStart = tick()
        while (tick() - tStart) < 3.2 do
            task.wait(0.08)
            local elapsed = tick() - tStart
            local serverConfirmed = (serverPreviewTick >= fireTick)
            if elapsed >= 0.65 and not retry1 and not serverConfirmed and not instantSpawned then
                retry1 = true
                if previewItemRemote then
                    pcall(function() previewItemRemote:FireServer(itemName) end)
                    if item.name ~= itemName then pcall(function() previewItemRemote:FireServer(item.name) end) end
                end
                triggerShopTryOnAnyWay(item.name, itemName)
            end
            if instantSpawned and instantSpawned.Parent and countBaseParts(instantSpawned) > 0 then
                targetInst = instantSpawned
                break
            end
            local fInst = findSpecificItemOnAvatar(itemName, itemCat, previewBeforeSet)
            if fInst then
                targetInst = fInst
                break
            end
        end
        for _, c in ipairs(childConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end

        if targetInst then
            waitForItemReady(targetInst, char, 1.5)
        else
            targetInst = tryDirectClientModelFallback(item, itemName, itemCat, true)
        end

        hideTryOnOverlays()
        releasePlayerMovement()
        activePreviewItem = item
        if updateInspBtnRef then updateInspBtnRef() end

        if targetInst then
            openItemColorAndToggles(item)
            if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("PREVIEW READY - Pick toggle/colors, then CLONE LOOK!", 2.8) end
            if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("Previewing! Tap CLONE LOOK when done", C.text) end
        else
            if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("Preview failed - try CLONE NOW", 2.0) end
            if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("Preview unavailable - try CLONE NOW", C.sub) end
        end
    end)

    return "OK"
end

-- Confirm & Clone active preview (locking ALL user-selected toggles & colors!)
confirmCloneRef = function(item)
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return end
    local targetItem = item or activePreviewItem
    if not targetItem then
        local n = lockCurrentAvatarLook("Accessories", nil)
        releasePlayerMovement()
        updateTabCounts()
        if refreshGridRef then refreshGridRef() end
        if n > 0 and notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("CLONED LOOK!", 2.2) end
        return
    end

    -- 1. Check if staged in RH_PreviewStage_<UserId> (Custom .rbxm, Preview Wings, or CantTryOn fallback!)
    local pf = getPreviewFolder(false)
    if pf and #pf:GetChildren() > 0 then
        local targetNorm = tostring(targetItem.assetName or targetItem.name or ""):lower():gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
        local pm = nil
        for _, ch in ipairs(pf:GetChildren()) do
            local cn = tostring(ch:GetAttribute("RH_ItemName") or ch.Name):lower():gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
            if cn == targetNorm or #pf:GetChildren() == 1 then
                pm = ch
                break
            end
        end
        if pm then
            local sk = pm:GetAttribute("RH_SlotKey") or pm.Name
            local bf = getBakedFolder(true)
            local old = bf:FindFirstChild(sk)
            if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end

            if activeHaloBobConns["Preview_" .. sk] then
                if activeHaloBobConns[sk] then pcall(function() activeHaloBobConns[sk]:Disconnect() end) end
                activeHaloBobConns[sk] = activeHaloBobConns["Preview_" .. sk]
                activeHaloBobConns["Preview_" .. sk] = nil
            end
            if hiddenBodyPartsBySlot["Preview_" .. sk] then
                hiddenBodyPartsBySlot[sk] = hiddenBodyPartsBySlot["Preview_" .. sk]
                hiddenBodyPartsBySlot["Preview_" .. sk] = nil
            end

            pm:SetAttribute("RH_PreviewItem", nil)
            pm:SetAttribute("RH_BakedLayer", true)
            pm.Parent = bf
            enforceHiddenBodyParts()
            bakedLayersBackup[sk] = deepClone(pm)

            activePreviewItem = nil
            previewBeforeSet = {}
            keepTogglesOpenUntil = 0
            if hideCustomToggleBarRef then hideCustomToggleBarRef() end
            releasePlayerMovement()
            updateTabCounts()
            if refreshGridRef then refreshGridRef() end
            if updateInspBtnRef then updateInspBtnRef() end
            local dName = pm:GetAttribute("RH_ItemName") or targetItem.name
            if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("CLONED: " .. tostring(dName) .. "!", 2.2) end
            if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("Cloned & Locked!", C.text) end
            return
        end
    end

    -- 2. If user is previewing a live Royale High server item (with chosen Toggle & Paint/Colors):
    local itemName = targetItem.assetName or targetItem.name
    local itemCat  = targetItem.cat
    local liveTarget = findSpecificItemOnAvatar(itemName, itemCat, previewBeforeSet)
    if not liveTarget then
        for _, fi in ipairs(findRoyaleHighItemsOnAvatar()) do
            if not previewBeforeSet[fi] and countBaseParts(fi) > 0 then
                liveTarget = fi
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if not liveTarget then
        liveTarget = findSpecificItemOnAvatar(itemName, itemCat, nil)
    end

    if liveTarget and countBaseParts(liveTarget) > 0 then
        local baked = bakeItemToAvatar(liveTarget, itemCat, nil, targetItem, false)
        if baked then
            local sk = baked:GetAttribute("RH_SlotKey") or baked.Name
            if savedColorsBySlot[sk] then
                for gIdx, cEntry in pairs(savedColorsBySlot[sk]) do
                    applyDirectColorToItem({name=targetItem.name, assetName=itemName, cat=itemCat, slotKey=sk}, cEntry.c3, gIdx, cEntry.tex)
                end
                bakedLayersBackup[sk] = deepClone(baked)
            end
        end

        activePreviewItem = nil
        previewBeforeSet = {}
        keepTogglesOpenUntil = 0
        pcall(function()
            local hud = pg:FindFirstChild("HUD")
            local ptf = hud and hud:FindFirstChild("PreviewTogglesFrame", true)
            if ptf then ptf.Visible = false end
        end)
        if exitPreviewRemote and serverPreviewActive then
            task.delay(0.05, function()
                pcall(function() exitPreviewRemote:FireServer() end)
            end)
        end
        enforceHiddenBodyParts()
        task.delay(0.18, enforceHiddenBodyParts)
        task.delay(0.45, enforceHiddenBodyParts)
        hideTryOnOverlays()
        releasePlayerMovement()
        updateTabCounts()
        if refreshGridRef then refreshGridRef() end
        if updateInspBtnRef then updateInspBtnRef() end
        if baked then
            if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("CLONED: " .. targetItem.name .. "!", 2.2) end
            if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("Cloned & Locked!", C.text) end
        else
            if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("Clone failed - tap CLONE NOW", 2.0) end
        end
        return
    end

    -- 3. No active preview found: quick-clone targetItem directly in 1 tap!
    activePreviewItem = nil
    previewBeforeSet = {}
    if updateInspBtnRef then updateInspBtnRef() end
    equipItem(targetItem)
end

-- ============================================================
-- 5. COMPACT MOBILE BLACK & WHITE GUI (270px x 380px)
-- ============================================================
local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
ScreenGui.Name           = "RH_Wardrobe_v55"
ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn   = false
ScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
ScreenGui.DisplayOrder   = 999
pcall(function() ScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling end)
do
	local okP = pcall(function() ScreenGui.Parent = getGuiParent() end)
	if not okP or not ScreenGui.Parent then
		ScreenGui.Parent = pg
	end
end

local function mk(cls, props, parent)
	local o = Instance.new(cls)
	for k, v in pairs(props) do o[k] = v end
	if parent then o.Parent = parent end
	return o
end

local function corner(r, parent)
	return mk("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r)}, parent)
end

local function stroke(t, c, parent)
	return mk("UIStroke", {Thickness = t, Color = c, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}, parent)
end

local function onClick(btn, fn)
	local lastT = 0
	local function fire()
		local now = tick()
		if (now - lastT) < 0.08 then return end
		lastT = now
		fn()
	end
	btn.Activated:Connect(fire)
	btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(fire)
end

		toastGen = toastGen + 1
		local myGen = toastGen
		UI.ToastText.Text = tostring(msg or "")
		UI.ToastFrame.Visible = true
		task.delay(dur or 2.2, function()
			if toastGen == myGen and UI.ToastFrame then
				UI.ToastFrame.Visible = false
			end
		end)
	end
end

local ToggleBtn = mk("TextButton", {
	Size              = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 30),
	Position          = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -46),
	BackgroundColor3  = C.bg,
	Text              = "CLOSET",
	TextSize          = 9,
	Font              = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
	TextColor3        = C.text,
	BorderSizePixel   = 0,
	ZIndex            = 100,
	AutoButtonColor   = false,
}, ScreenGui)

stroke(1, C.borderHi, ToggleBtn)

-- Draggable Toggle Button on mobile
do
	local btnDragging, btnDragStart, btnStartPos
	ToggleBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			btnDragging  = true
			btnDragStart = input.Position
			btnStartPos  = ToggleBtn.Position
		end
	end)
	ToggleBtn.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if btnDragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			local delta = input.Position - btnDragStart
			ToggleBtn.Position = UDim2.new(btnStartPos.X.Scale, btnStartPos.X.Offset + delta.X, btnStartPos.Y.Scale, btnStartPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
		end
	end)
	ToggleBtn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			btnDragging = false
		end
	end)
end

-- Compact Mobile Window: 270px W x 380px H
UI.W, UI.H = 270, 380
local Main = mk("Frame", {
	Size             = UDim2.new(0, UI.W, 0, UI.H),
	Position         = UDim2.new(0.5, -UI.W/2, 0.5, -UI.H/2),
	BackgroundColor3 = C.bg,
	BorderSizePixel  = 0,
	Visible          = false,
	ZIndex           = 10,
	ClipsDescendants = false,
}, ScreenGui)
corner(8, Main)
stroke(1, C.border, Main)

local function updateToggleBtnState()
    if Main.Visible then
        ToggleBtn.Text = "CLOSE"
        ToggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.tabActive
        ToggleBtn.TextColor3 = C.tabActTxt
    else
        ToggleBtn.Text = "CLOSET"
        ToggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.bg
        ToggleBtn.TextColor3 = C.text
    end
end

local closeInspRef = nil

do
	local lastToggleTick = 0
	onClick(ToggleBtn, function()
		local now = tick()
		if now - lastToggleTick < 0.25 then return end
		lastToggleTick = now

		Main.Visible = not Main.Visible
		updateToggleBtnState()
		if not Main.Visible and closeInspRef then
			closeInspRef()
		end
	end)
end

-- Title Bar (Window drag attached strictly to TitleBar so CatScroll/GridScroll clicks are NEVER swallowed!)
UI.TitleBar = mk("Frame", {
	Size             = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26),
	BackgroundColor3 = C.panel,
	BorderSizePixel  = 0,
	Active           = true,
	ZIndex           = 11,
}, Main)
do
	local dragging, dragStart, startPos
	UI.TitleBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging  = true
			dragStart = input.Position
			startPos  = Main.Position
			pcall(function()
				input.Changed:Connect(function()
					if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
						dragging = false
					end
				end)
			end)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			local delta = input.Position - dragStart
			Main.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
		end
	end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- 6. GRID POPULATION & CATEGORY NAVIGATION
-- ============================================================
local currentCat    = "Heels"
local currentSearch = ""
local cardInstances = {}

local function makeCard(item, order)
	local safeName = tostring(item.name or item.assetName or "Item")
	local safeImg = (type(item.img) == "string") and item.img or ""
	local col = (order - 1) % 3
	local row = math.floor((order - 1) / 3)
	local card = mk("TextButton", {
		Name             = "Card_" .. safeName:sub(1, 10),
		Size             = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 68),
		Position         = UDim2.new(0, 4 + col * 62, 0, 4 + row * 72),
		BackgroundColor3 = C.card,
		Text             = "",
		BorderSizePixel  = 0,
		Visible          = true,
		Active           = true,
		AutoButtonColor  = true,
		LayoutOrder      = order,
		ZIndex           = 13,
	}, GridScroll)
	corner(4, card)
	stroke(1, C.border, card)

	local img = mk("ImageLabel", {
		Size             = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 38),
		Position         = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 3),
		BackgroundColor3 = C.btn,
		BorderSizePixel  = 0,
		ScaleType        = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
		Image            = safeImg,
		ZIndex           = 14,
	}, card)
	corner(3, img)

	if safeImg == "" then
		if item.cat == "Wings" then
			_G.RH_WingModelCache = _G.RH_WingModelCache or {}
			local phLbl = mk("TextLabel", {
				Size               = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Text               = "...",
				TextColor3         = C.muted,
				Font               = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
				TextSize           = 7,
				ZIndex             = 15,
			}, img)
			task.spawn(function()
				local wingKey = item.assetName or item.name
				local tmpl = _G.RH_WingModelCache[wingKey]
				if tmpl == nil then
					local getWingFn = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GetWingModel")
					if getWingFn and getWingFn:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
						local ok, res = pcall(function() return getWingFn:InvokeServer(wingKey) end)
						tmpl = (ok and typeof(res) == "Instance") and res or false
						_G.RH_WingModelCache[wingKey] = tmpl
					else
						_G.RH_WingModelCache[wingKey] = false
					end
				end
				if tmpl and typeof(tmpl) == "Instance" and img and img.Parent then
					local clone = tmpl:Clone()
					local vpf = mk("ViewportFrame", {
						Size                   = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
						BackgroundTransparency = 1,
						Ambient                = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230),
						LightColor             = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
						LightDirection         = Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1),
						ZIndex                 = 15,
					}, img)
					local wm = Instance.new("WorldModel")
					wm.Parent = vpf
					local cam = Instance.new("Camera")
					cam.FieldOfView = 36
					cam.Parent = vpf
					vpf.CurrentCamera = cam
					clone.Parent = wm
					local cf, sz
					if clone:IsA("Model") then
						cf, sz = clone:GetBoundingBox()
					else
						local bp = clone:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
						cf = bp and bp.CFrame or CFrame.new()
						sz = bp and bp.Size or Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
					end
					local center = cf.Position
					local spanW, lookDir
					if sz.X >= sz.Z then
						spanW = math.max(sz.X, sz.Y)
						lookDir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
					else
						spanW = math.max(sz.Z, sz.Y)
						lookDir = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
					end
					local dist = (spanW * 0.62) / math.tan(math.rad(cam.FieldOfView * 0.5))
					cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(center + lookDir * math.max(dist, 1.8), center)
					if phLbl then phLbl.Visible = false end
				elseif phLbl and phLbl.Parent then
					phLbl.Text = "WING"
				end
			end)
		else
			mk("TextLabel", {
				Size               = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Text               = item.isWorn and "WORN" or "NO IMG",
				TextColor3         = item.isWorn and C.text or C.muted,
				Font               = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
				TextSize           = 7,
				ZIndex             = 15,
			}, img)
		end
	end

	mk("TextLabel", {
		Size               = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 20),
		Position           = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 44),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text               = safeName,
		TextColor3         = C.text,
		Font               = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
		TextSize           = 6.5,
		TextWrapped        = true,
		TextYAlignment     = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
		ZIndex             = 14,
	}, card)

	onClick(card, function()
		if UI.openInsp then
			UI.openInsp(item)
		elseif notifyToastRef then
			notifyToastRef("Inspector loading...", 1.5)
		end
	end)

	cardInstances[item] = card
	return card
end

local function refreshGrid()
	local okRun, errRun = pcall(function()
		for _, card in pairs(cardInstances) do pcall(function() card:Destroy() end) end
		cardInstances = {}
		for _, ch in ipairs(GridScroll:GetChildren()) do
			if ch:IsA("GuiObject") then pcall(function() ch:Destroy() end) end
		end

		if currentCat == "Stats" then return end

		local list = {}
		if currentCat == "Worn" then
			local okW, wl = pcall(getWornItemsList)
			list = (okW and type(wl) == "table") and wl or {}
		else
			list = categories[currentCat] or {}
		end

		local search = (currentCat == "Worn") and "" or tostring(currentSearch or ""):lower()
		local filtered = {}
		for _, item in ipairs(list) do
			if type(item) == "table" then
				local nm = tostring(item.name or item.assetName or "")
				if search == "" or nm:lower():find(search, 1, true) then
					table.insert(filtered, item)
				end
			end
		end

		if #filtered == 0 then
			local emptyCard = mk("Frame", {
				Name             = "EmptyNotice",
				Size             = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 64),
				Position         = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4),
				BackgroundColor3 = C.card,
				BorderSizePixel  = 0,
				ZIndex           = 13,
			}, GridScroll)
			corner(4, emptyCard)
			stroke(1, C.border, emptyCard)

			local emptyMsg = (currentCat == "Worn")
				and "NO ITEMS WORN\n\nEquip items from Heels, Skirts, or Bodices to wear and stack them!"
				or ("No matching items in " .. tostring(currentCat) .. " (total=" .. tostring(#list) .. ")")
			mk("TextLabel", {
				Size               = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0),
				Position           = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Text               = emptyMsg,
				TextColor3         = C.sub,
				Font               = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
				TextSize           = 7.5,
				TextWrapped        = true,
				ZIndex             = 14,
			}, emptyCard)
			cardInstances[emptyCard] = emptyCard
			GridScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 76)
			return
		end

		if currentCat ~= "Worn" then
			-- Sort: Items with valid images FIRST, items with NO image LAST
			pcall(function()
				table.sort(filtered, function(a, b)
					local aHas = (type(a.img) == "string" and a.img ~= "") and 1 or 0
					local bHas = (type(b.img) == "string" and b.img ~= "") and 1 or 0
					if aHas ~= bHas then
						return aHas > bHas
					end
					return tostring(a.name or ""):lower() < tostring(b.name or ""):lower()
				end)
			end)
		end

		local firstCardErr = nil
		local madeCount = 0
		for order, item in ipairs(filtered) do
			local okC, errC = pcall(makeCard, item, order)
			if okC then
				madeCount = madeCount + 1
			elseif not firstCardErr then
				firstCardErr = tostring(errC)
			end
		end
		if madeCount == 0 and firstCardErr then
			local errCard = mk("Frame", {
				Name             = "GridErrNotice",
				Size             = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 68),
				Position         = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4),
				BackgroundColor3 = C.card,
				BorderSizePixel  = 0,
				ZIndex           = 15,
			}, GridScroll)
			corner(4, errCard)
			stroke(1, C.borderHi, errCard)
			mk("TextLabel", {
				Size               = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -4),
				Position           = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 2),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Text               = "CARD ERR: " .. firstCardErr,
				TextColor3         = C.text,
				Font               = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
				TextSize           = 7,
				TextWrapped        = true,
				ZIndex             = 16,
			}, errCard)
			cardInstances[errCard] = errCard
		end
		GridScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.ceil(math.max(1, madeCount) / 3) * 72 + 12)
	end)
	if not okRun then
		warn("[RH Wardrobe v55] refreshGrid error:", errRun)
		pcall(function() SearchBox.PlaceholderText = "ERR: " .. tostring(errRun):sub(1, 24) end)
		local errBox = mk("TextLabel", {
			Size               = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 64),
			Position           = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4),
			BackgroundColor3   = C.card,
			Text               = "GRID ERR: " .. tostring(errRun),
			TextColor3         = C.text,
			Font               = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
			TextSize           = 7.5,
			TextWrapped        = true,
			ZIndex             = 30,
		}, GridScroll)
		cardInstances[errBox] = errBox
	end
end

UI.updateTabCounts = updateTabCounts
refreshGridRef = function(targetCat)
    if not targetCat or targetCat == currentCat then
        refreshGrid()
    end
end
UI.refreshGrid = refreshGridRef

local function setActiveCategory(cat)
	currentCat = cat
	if cat == "Worn" and SearchBox.Text ~= "" then
		currentSearch = ""
		SearchBox.Text = ""
	end
	for c, btn in pairs(tabButtons) do
		if c == cat then
			btn.BackgroundColor3 = C.tabActive
			btn.TextColor3       = C.tabActTxt
		else
			btn.BackgroundColor3 = C.tabInact
			btn.TextColor3       = C.tabInaTxt
		end
	end
	if closeInspRef then pcall(closeInspRef) end
	pcall(updateTabCounts)

	if cat == "Stats" then
		SearchBoxFrame.Visible = false
		GridScroll.Visible     = false
		StatsPanel.Visible     = true
	else
		StatsPanel.Visible     = false
		SearchBoxFrame.Visible = true
		GridScroll.Visible     = true
		pcall(function() GridScroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0) end)
		refreshGrid()
	end
end

UI.setActiveCategory = setActiveCategory

-- Create sidebar category buttons wired DIRECTLY to setActiveCategory
for i, cat in ipairs(catOrder) do
	local count = #(categories[cat] or {})
	local btn = mk("TextButton", {
		Size             = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 30),
		BackgroundColor3 = C.tabInact,
		Text             = cat .. "\n(" .. count .. ")",
		TextColor3       = C.tabInaTxt,
		Font             = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
		TextSize         = 7.5,
		BorderSizePixel  = 0,
		LayoutOrder      = i,
		ZIndex           = 20,
		Active           = true,
		AutoButtonColor  = true,
	}, CatScroll)
	corner(4, btn)
	stroke(1, C.border, btn)
	tabButtons[cat] = btn
	onClick(btn, function()
		local okSw, errSw = pcall(setActiveCategory, cat)
		if not okSw and notifyToastRef then
			notifyToastRef("Tab err: " .. tostring(errSw), 3.5)
		end
	end)
end

onClick(TitleStatsBtn, function()
	pcall(setActiveCategory, "Stats")
end)

SearchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
	currentSearch = SearchBox.Text
	pcall(refreshGrid)
end)

Main.Visible = true
updateToggleBtnState()
local okInit, errInit = pcall(setActiveCategory, "Heels")
if not okInit then
	warn("[RH Wardrobe v55] Grid init err:", errInit)
	if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("Grid init err: " .. tostring(errInit), 5.0) end
else
	local hCnt = #(categories.Heels or {})
	if notifyToastRef then notifyToastRef("Wardrobe Ready (" .. tostring(hCnt) .. " Heels)", 2.2) end
end
pcall(updateTabCounts)

do
local okInsp, errInsp = pcall(function()
-- Slide-up Inspector (140px height)
local InspHeight = 140
local Inspector = mk("Frame", {
	Size             = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, InspHeight),
	Position         = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
	BackgroundColor3 = C.panel,
	BorderSizePixel  = 0,
	Visible          = false,
	ZIndex           = 25,
	ClipsDescendants = true,
}, Main)
corner(8, Inspector)
stroke(1, C.borderHi, Inspector)
