local selectedItem = nil
local CustomToggleBar = nil
local function normItemKey(it)
	if not it then return "" end
	return tostring(it.assetName or it.name or ""):lower():gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
end
local function isItemInPreview(it)
	if not it or it.isWorn then return false end
	local k = normItemKey(it)
	if k == "" then return false end
	if activePreviewItem and normItemKey(activePreviewItem) == k then
		return true
	end
	local pf = getPreviewFolder(false)
	if pf then
		for _, ch in ipairs(pf:GetChildren()) do
			local cn = tostring(ch:GetAttribute("RH_ItemName") or ch.Name):lower():gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
			if cn == k then return true end
		end
	end
	return false
end
updateInspBtnRef = function()
	if not selectedItem then return end
	if selectedItem.isWorn then
		inspPrice.Text = "Equipped: " .. tostring(selectedItem.cat or "Custom")
		equipBtn.Text = "UNEQUIP / REMOVE"
		equipBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.card
		equipBtn.TextColor3 = C.text
	elseif isItemInPreview(selectedItem) then
		local pr = tostring(selectedItem.price or "")
		inspPrice.Text = "Previewing! " .. (pr ~= "" and ("(" .. pr .. ")") or "(Ready to Clone)")
		equipBtn.Text = "CLONE LOOK"
		equipBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.btnHi
		equipBtn.TextColor3 = C.btnTextHi
	else
		local pr = tostring(selectedItem.price or "")
		inspPrice.Text = (pr ~= "" and ("Price: " .. pr) or "Price: Special")
		equipBtn.Text = "PREVIEW FIRST"
		equipBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.btnHi
		equipBtn.TextColor3 = C.btnTextHi
	end
end

local function closeInsp()
	Inspector.Visible = false
	Inspector.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
	if CustomToggleBar then CustomToggleBar.Visible = false end
end
closeInspRef = closeInsp
onClick(inspClose, closeInsp)

local function openInsp(item)
	selectedItem = item
	inspName.Text = tostring(item.name or "ITEM"):upper()
	updateInspBtnRef()
	if item.img and item.img ~= "" then
		inspImg.Image = item.img
		inspPlaceholder.Visible = false
	else
		inspImg.Image = ""
		inspPlaceholder.Visible = true
	end
	Inspector.Visible = true
	Inspector.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -InspHeight)
end
UI.openInsp = openInsp

local function handleWearOrRemove()
	if not selectedItem then return end
	if selectedItem.isWorn then
		setStatus("Unequipping...", C.sub)
		unequipWornItem(selectedItem)
		setStatus("Item Removed!", C.text)
		closeInsp()
		updateTabCounts()
		if refreshGridRef then refreshGridRef("Worn") end
	elseif isItemInPreview(selectedItem) then
		setStatus("Cloning customized look...", C.sub)
		if confirmCloneRef then confirmCloneRef(selectedItem) end
	else
		setStatus("Starting Preview...", C.sub)
		if startPreviewRef then startPreviewRef(selectedItem) end
	end
end
onClick(equipBtn, handleWearOrRemove)

-- Compact scrollable toggle selector popup for ALL 16 custom .rbxm models
CustomToggleBar = mk("Frame", {
	Name             = "CustomToggleBar",
	Size             = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 32),
	Position         = UDim2.new(0, 6, 1, -174),
	BackgroundColor3 = C.panel,
	BorderSizePixel  = 0,
	Visible          = false,
	ZIndex           = 35,
}, Main)
corner(4, CustomToggleBar)
stroke(1, C.borderHi, CustomToggleBar)

local CustomToggleScroll = mk("ScrollingFrame", {
	Size               = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, 0),
	Position           = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 0),
	BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	BorderSizePixel    = 0,
	ScrollBarThickness = 2,
	CanvasSize         = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
	ZIndex             = 36,
}, CustomToggleBar)
pcall(function() CustomToggleScroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X end)
pcall(function() CustomToggleScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X end)
mk("UIListLayout", {
	FillDirection       = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
	Padding             = UDim.new(0, 4),
	VerticalAlignment   = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,
}, CustomToggleScroll)

local closeCustomTBtn = mk("TextButton", {
	Size             = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 22),
	Position         = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0.5, -11),
	BackgroundColor3 = C.card,
	Text             = "X",
	TextColor3       = C.sub,
	Font             = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
	TextSize         = 7,
	BorderSizePixel  = 0,
	ZIndex           = 37,
}, CustomToggleBar)
corner(3, closeCustomTBtn)
onClick(closeCustomTBtn, function() CustomToggleBar.Visible = false end)
hideCustomToggleBarRef = function()
	if CustomToggleBar then CustomToggleBar.Visible = false end
end

local function openCustomToggleBarFor(item)
	for _, ch in ipairs(CustomToggleScroll:GetChildren()) do
		if ch:IsA("GuiObject") then ch:Destroy() end
	end
	local pf = getPreviewFolder(false)
	local bf = getBakedFolder(false)
	local meta, canonName, b64Key = findCustomRbxmSpec(item)
	if (not pf and not bf) or not meta then
		CustomToggleBar.Visible = false
		return
	end
	local cat = item.cat or meta.cat
	local cleanCanon = tostring(canonName or meta.name or item.name):gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "")
	local expectedSlot = ((cat == "Halos") and "Halo_" or "Acc_") .. cleanCanon
	local mdl = nil
	for _, folder in ipairs({pf, bf}) do
		if folder then
			mdl = (item.slotKey and folder:FindFirstChild(item.slotKey)) or folder:FindFirstChild(expectedSlot)
			if not mdl and b64Key then
				for _, ch in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
					if ch:GetAttribute("RH_CustomB64Key") == b64Key then
						mdl = ch
						break
					end
				end
			end
			if mdl then break end
		end
	end
	if not mdl then CustomToggleBar.Visible = false return end

	-- Group toggle folders by name across multi-limb models & nested toggle models (e.g., Lucky 21 Beams, Winter 22 Crystal Sparkles!)
	local groupList = {}
	local groupMap = {}
	local skipNames = {
		["Configuration"]=true, ["Colors"]=true, ["InteractSettings"]=true, ["CycleVariants"]=true, ["1"]=true,
		["Size: 2"]=true, ["Orientation: Down"]=true, ["Turn to Brooch"]=true,
	}

	for _, ch in ipairs(mdl:GetDescendants()) do
		if ch:IsA("Folder") and not skipNames[ch.Name] and not ch.Name:match("CRFolder$") and not ch.Name:match("^Cycle") and not ch.Name:match("^Wand Rotation") and not ch.Name:match("^Wand Size") then
			if not ch:FindFirstAncestor("Configuration") and not ch:FindFirstAncestor("Colors") then
				local hasToggleContent = false
				for _, dc in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do
					if dc:IsA("BasePart") or dc:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or dc:IsA("PointLight") or dc:IsA("Attachment") or dc:IsA("Beam") or dc:IsA("Trail") then
						hasToggleContent = true
						break
					elseif dc:IsA("Model") and dc:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) then
						hasToggleContent = true
						break
					end
				end
				if hasToggleContent then
					if not groupMap[ch.Name] then
						groupMap[ch.Name] = {}
						table.insert(groupList, { name = ch.Name, folders = groupMap[ch.Name] })
					end
					table.insert(groupMap[ch.Name], ch)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if #groupList == 0 then CustomToggleBar.Visible = false return end

	local function isGroupActive(fldList)
		local checkedAny = false
		for _, fld in ipairs(fldList) do
			for _, p in ipairs(fld:GetDescendants()) do
				if p:IsA("BasePart") and (p:GetAttribute("RH_OrigTransparency") or 0) < 0.99 then
					checkedAny = true
					if p.Transparency < 0.99 then return true end
				elseif p:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or p:IsA("PointLight") or p:IsA("Beam") or p:IsA("Trail") then
					checkedAny = true
					if p.Enabled then return true end
				end
			end
		end
		return checkedAny
	end

	for _, grp in ipairs(groupList) do
		local isOn = isGroupActive(grp.folders)
		local tb = mk("TextButton", {
			Size             = UDim2.new(0, 74, 0, 22),
			BackgroundColor3 = isOn and C.accent or C.card,
			Text             = grp.name:sub(1, 20),
			TextWrapped      = true,
			TextColor3       = isOn and C.accentTxt or C.sub,
			Font             = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
			TextSize         = 6,
			BorderSizePixel  = 0,
			ZIndex           = 36,
		}, CustomToggleScroll)
		corner(3, tb)
		tb.Activated:Connect(function()
			local newOn = not isGroupActive(grp.folders)
			for _, fld in ipairs(grp.folders) do
				for _, p in ipairs(fld:GetDescendants()) do
					if p:IsA("BasePart") then
						p.Transparency = newOn and (p:GetAttribute("RH_OrigTransparency") or 0) or 1
					elseif p:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or p:IsA("PointLight") or p:IsA("Beam") or p:IsA("Trail") then
						p.Enabled = newOn
					end
				end
			end
			tb.BackgroundColor3 = newOn and C.accent or C.card
			tb.TextColor3 = newOn and C.accentTxt or C.sub
			if mdl:GetAttribute("RH_BakedLayer") then
				bakedLayersBackup[mdl.Name] = deepClone(mdl)
			end
		end)
	end
	CustomToggleBar.Visible = true
end
UI.openCustomToggleBarFor = openCustomToggleBarFor

local function handleColor()
	if not selectedItem then return end
	if not selectedItem.isWorn and not isItemInPreview(selectedItem) then
		-- Auto-start preview first so user can pick toggles & colors BEFORE cloning!
		local bf = getBakedFolder(false)
		local cleanC = normItemKey(selectedItem)
		local foundBaked = false
		if bf then
			for _, ch in ipairs(bf:GetChildren()) do
				if tostring(ch:GetAttribute("RH_ItemName") or ch.Name):lower():gsub("%s*%b()%s*", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "") == cleanC then
					foundBaked = true
					break
				end
			end
		end
		if not foundBaked and startPreviewRef then
			setStatus("Previewing to customize...", C.sub)
			startPreviewRef(selectedItem)
			return
		end
	end
	local ok = openItemColorAndToggles(selectedItem)
	if findCustomRbxmSpec(selectedItem) then
		openCustomToggleBarFor(selectedItem)
	else
		CustomToggleBar.Visible = false
	end
	if ok then
		setStatus("Toggles & Colors opened!", C.text)
	else
		setStatus("Colors/Toggles opened", C.sub)
	end
end
onClick(colorBtn, handleColor)

onClick(bakeBtn, function()
	if confirmCloneRef and (isItemInPreview(selectedItem) or selectedItem) then
		setStatus("Cloning & Locking...", C.sub)
		confirmCloneRef(selectedItem)
		return
	end
	local cat = selectedItem and selectedItem.cat or "Accessories"
	local n = lockCurrentAvatarLook(cat, selectedItem)
	releasePlayerMovement()
	if n > 0 then
		setStatus("Layer Locked & Saved!", C.text)
	else
		setStatus("Current Look Baked!", C.text)
	end
	updateTabCounts()
	if refreshGridRef then refreshGridRef() end
end)

onClick(clearLayersBtn, function()
	clearAllWornLayers()
	setStatus("Cleared all layers.", C.sub)
	updateTabCounts()
	if refreshGridRef then refreshGridRef() end
end)
end)
if not okInsp then warn("[RH Wardrobe v55] Inspector init err:", errInsp) end
end -- Inspector & CustomToggleBar scope

local function formatCommas(str)
	local numStr = str:gsub("[^%d]", "")
	if numStr == "" then return "" end
	local k
	while true do
		numStr, k = string.gsub(numStr, "^(-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", '%1,%2')
		if k == 0 then break end
	end
	return numStr
end

local activeSpoofs = {}

local function createStatRow(labelText, defaultVal, callback)
	local row = mk("Frame", {
		Size             = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42),
		BackgroundColor3 = C.bg,
		BorderSizePixel  = 0,
		ZIndex           = 14,
	}, StatsScroll)
	corner(4, row)
	stroke(1, C.border, row)

	mk("TextLabel", {
		Size               = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 14),
		Position           = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 3),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text               = labelText:upper(),
		TextColor3         = C.sub,
		Font               = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
		TextSize           = 7.5,
		TextXAlignment     = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		ZIndex             = 15,
	}, row)

	local box = mk("TextBox", {
		Size               = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 20),
		Position           = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 18),
		BackgroundColor3   = C.card,
		Text               = defaultVal,
		PlaceholderText    = "Enter amount...",
		TextColor3         = C.text,
		PlaceholderColor3  = C.muted,
		Font               = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
		TextSize           = 8.5,
		BorderSizePixel    = 0,
		ClearTextOnFocus   = false,
		ZIndex             = 15,
	}, row)
	corner(3, box)
	stroke(1, C.border, box)

	box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		local raw       = box.Text
		local formatted = formatCommas(raw)
		if raw ~= formatted then box.Text = formatted end
		activeSpoofs[labelText] = formatted
		callback(formatted)
	end)
end

local cachedStatLabels = { Diamonds = {}, Level = {}, Candy = {}, Cash = {} }
local lastStatScanTick = 0

local function rebuildStatLabelCache(force)
	local now = tick()
	if not force and (now - lastStatScanTick) < 10 then return end
	lastStatScanTick = now

	cachedStatLabels = { Diamonds = {}, Level = {}, Candy = {}, Cash = {} }
	local roots = {}
	for _, gui in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
		if gui.Name == "Shop" then
			if gui:FindFirstChild("Header") then table.insert(roots, gui.Header) end
		elseif gui.Name ~= "RH_Wardrobe_v55" then
			table.insert(roots, gui)
		end
	end
	for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
	for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("TextLabel") then
			local nl = d.Name:lower()
			local fn = d:GetFullName():lower()
			if not fn:find("rh_wardrobe") then
				if nl:find("diamondamount") or nl:find("diamondcount") then
					table.insert(cachedStatLabels.Diamonds, d)
				elseif nl:find("candyamount") or nl:find("candycount") then
					table.insert(cachedStatLabels.Candy, d)
				elseif nl:find("cashamount") or nl:find("cloudcash") or nl:find("dailyloginrewardscurrency") then
					table.insert(cachedStatLabels.Cash, d)
				elseif (nl:find("level") or fn:find("level")) and not fn:find("quality") and not fn:find("volume") and not fn:find("unlock") then
					table.insert(cachedStatLabels.Level, d)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	end
end
task.spawn(function()
	task.wait(0.5)
	pcall(rebuildStatLabelCache, true)
end)

local function updateDiamonds(val)
	if val == "" then return end
	rebuildStatLabelCache(false)
	local num = tonumber((val:gsub("[^%d]", "")))
	pcall(function() pg.Shop.Header.diamondamount.Text = val end)
	pcall(function() pg.HUD.Frame.Middle.DiamondsFrame.DiamondAmount.Text = val end)
	pcall(function() pg.MermaidShopDiamondCount.Frame.DiamondAmount.Text = val end)
	for _, d in ipairs(cachedStatLabels.Diamonds) do
		if d and d.Parent then pcall(function() d.Text = val end) end
	end
	if num then
		pcall(function()
			local ls = lp:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
			if ls and ls:FindFirstChild("Diamonds") then ls.Diamonds.Value = num end
		end)
	end
end

local function updateLevel(val)
	if val == "" then return end
	rebuildStatLabelCache(false)
	local rawNumStr = val:gsub("[^%d]", "")
	local num = tonumber(rawNumStr)
	if num then
		pcall(function()
			local ls = lp:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
			if ls and ls:FindFirstChild("Level") then ls.Level.Value = num end
		end)
	end
	for _, d in ipairs(cachedStatLabels.Level) do
		if d and d.Parent then
			local txt = d.Text or ""
			if txt:match("^Grade Level:%s*%d+") then
				pcall(function() d.Text = "Grade Level: " .. rawNumStr end)
			elseif txt:match("^Level:%s*%d+") then
				pcall(function() d.Text = "Level: " .. rawNumStr end)
			elseif txt:match("^Level%s+[%d,]+") then
				pcall(function() d.Text = "Level " .. val end)
			elseif txt:match("^[Ll][Vv][Ll]?%.?%s*[%d,]+") then
				pcall(function() d.Text = "Lv. " .. val end)
			elseif tonumber(txt:gsub("[^%d]", "")) then
				pcall(function() d.Text = val end)
			end
		end
	end
	if lp.Character then
		for _, d in ipairs(lp.Character:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("TextLabel") then
				local nl = d.Name:lower()
				local txt = d.Text or ""
				if (nl:find("level") or (d.Parent and d.Parent.Name:lower():find("level"))) and tonumber(txt:gsub("[^%d]", "")) then
					if txt:match("^Level%s+") then
						pcall(function() d.Text = "Level " .. val end)
					else
						pcall(function() d.Text = val end)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

local function updateCandy(val)
	if val == "" then return end
	rebuildStatLabelCache(false)
	pcall(function() pg.Shop.Header.candyamount.Text = val end)
	pcall(function() pg.HUD.Frame.HalloweenCandy.CandyAmount.Text = val end)
	for _, d in ipairs(cachedStatLabels.Candy) do
		if d and d.Parent then pcall(function() d.Text = val end) end
	end
end

local function updateCash(val)
	if val == "" then return end
	rebuildStatLabelCache(false)
	for _, d in ipairs(cachedStatLabels.Cash) do
		if d and d.Parent then pcall(function() d.Text = val end) end
	end
end

createStatRow("Diamonds",   "1,000,000", updateDiamonds)
createStatRow("Level",      "500",       updateLevel)
createStatRow("Candy",      "50,000",    updateCandy)
createStatRow("Cloud Cash", "10,000",    updateCash)

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		task.wait(0.8)
		if activeSpoofs["Diamonds"]   then updateDiamonds(activeSpoofs["Diamonds"])   end
		if activeSpoofs["Level"]      then updateLevel(activeSpoofs["Level"])          end
		if activeSpoofs["Candy"]      then updateCandy(activeSpoofs["Candy"])          end
		if activeSpoofs["Cloud Cash"] then updateCash(activeSpoofs["Cloud Cash"])      end
	end
end)

-- Client-side visual "Card Accepted!" + Receipt animation for Diamonds, Candy, and Cloud Cash
task.spawn(function()
	local hookedPurchaseGui = false
	while not hookedPurchaseGui do
		local pGuiRoot = pg:FindFirstChild("PurchaseGui")
		local pFailedDiag = pGuiRoot and pGuiRoot:FindFirstChild("PurchaseFailed", true)
		local pGui = pFailedDiag and pFailedDiag.Parent
		if pGui then
			hookedPurchaseGui = true
			local function hookCurrencyFail(failFrame, statKey, updateFn)
				if not failFrame then return end
				local animGen = 0
				local lastTriggerTick = 0
				failFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
					if not failFrame.Visible then return end
					local now = tick()
					if (now - lastTriggerTick) < 0.25 then return end
					lastTriggerTick = now

					local spoofStr = activeSpoofs[statKey]
					if not spoofStr or spoofStr == "" then return end
					local spoofBal = tonumber((spoofStr:gsub("[^%d]", ""))) or 0

					local priceLbl = pGui:FindFirstChild("Receipt")
					             and pGui.Receipt:FindFirstChild("PriceSplit")
					             and pGui.Receipt.PriceSplit:FindFirstChild("Price")
					local priceNum = priceLbl and tonumber((priceLbl.Text:gsub("[^%d]", ""))) or 0
					if spoofBal < priceNum then return end

					animGen = animGen + 1
					local myGen = animGen
					failFrame.Visible = false

					task.spawn(function()
						local newBal = math.max(0, spoofBal - priceNum)
						local formattedNew = formatCommas(tostring(newBal))
						activeSpoofs[statKey] = formattedNew
						updateFn(formattedNew)

						-- Update client-side "Owned: X" visual badge in Shop if visible
						pcall(function()
							local itemNameLbl = pGui:FindFirstChild("ItemName", true) or (pGui:FindFirstChild("Receipt") and pGui.Receipt:FindFirstChild("ItemName", true))
							local boughtName = itemNameLbl and itemNameLbl:IsA("TextLabel") and itemNameLbl.Text or ""
							local shop = pg:FindFirstChild("Shop")
							if shop and boughtName ~= "" then
								for _, lbl in ipairs(shop:GetDescendants()) do
									if lbl:IsA("TextLabel") and lbl.Visible then
										local tl = (lbl.Text or ""):lower()
										if tl:match("^owned:?%s*%d+") or tl == "not owned" then
											local card = lbl.Parent
											local hasMatch = false
											for _ = 1, 4 do
												if not card then break end
												for _, ch in ipairs(card:GetDescendants()) do
													if ch:IsA("TextLabel") and ch.Text == boughtName then
														hasMatch = true
														break
													end
												end
												if hasMatch then break end
												card = card.Parent
											end
											if hasMatch then
												local curOwned = tonumber((tl:gsub("[^%d]", ""))) or 0
												lbl.Text = "Owned: " .. tostring(curOwned + 1)
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end)

						pcall(function()
							local s = pGui:FindFirstChild("Success") or pGuiRoot:FindFirstChild("Success", true)
							if s and s:IsA("Sound") then s:Play() end
						end)

						local pAcc = pGui:FindFirstChild("PurchaseAccepted")
						if pAcc then
							pAcc.Visible = true
							if pAcc:FindFirstChild("MainText") then
								pAcc.MainText.Text = "Card Accepted!"
							end
							for i = 1, 5 do
								local f = pAcc:FindFirstChild(tostring(i))
								if f and f:IsA("GuiObject") then f.Visible = true end
								if i > 1 then
									local prev = pAcc:FindFirstChild(tostring(i - 1))
									if prev and prev:IsA("GuiObject") then prev.Visible = false end
								end
								task.wait(0.15)
							end
							if pAcc:FindFirstChild("MainText") then
								pAcc.MainText.Text = "Purchase Complete!"
							end
						end

						pcall(function()
							local cSnd = pGui:FindFirstChild("Complete") or pGuiRoot:FindFirstChild("Complete", true)
							if cSnd and cSnd:IsA("Sound") then cSnd:Play() end
						end)

						local receipt = pGui:FindFirstChild("Receipt")
						if receipt then
							receipt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.275, 0)
							receipt.Visible = true
							pcall(function()
								receipt:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.72, 0), Enum.EasingDirection.Out, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, 0.45, true)
							end)
						end

						task.wait(2.2)
						if animGen ~= myGen then return end
						if pAcc then pAcc.Visible = false end
						if receipt then
							receipt.Visible = false
							receipt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.275, 0)
						end
						if pGui:FindFirstChild("PurchaseScreen") then
							pGui.PurchaseScreen.Visible = true
						end
						if pGuiRoot and pGuiRoot:IsA("ScreenGui") then
							pGuiRoot.Enabled = false
						end
					end)
				end)
			end

			hookCurrencyFail(pGui:FindFirstChild("PurchaseFailed"), "Diamonds", updateDiamonds)
			hookCurrencyFail(pGui:FindFirstChild("PurchaseFailedCandy"), "Candy", updateCandy)
			hookCurrencyFail(pGui:FindFirstChild("PurchaseFailedDailyLoginRewards"), "Cloud Cash", updateCash)
			-- Also catch generic currency fail frames if named differently
			for _, ch in ipairs(pGui:GetChildren()) do
				local cnl = ch.Name:lower()
				if cnl:find("purchasefailed") and ch.Name ~= "PurchaseFailed" and ch.Name ~= "PurchaseFailedCandy" and ch.Name ~= "PurchaseFailedDailyLoginRewards" then
					if cnl:find("candy") then
						hookCurrencyFail(ch, "Candy", updateCandy)
					elseif cnl:find("cash") or cnl:find("cloud") or cnl:find("daily") then
						hookCurrencyFail(ch, "Cloud Cash", updateCash)
					end
				end
			end
		end
		task.wait(1)
	end
end)

end)
if not okStat then warn("[RH Wardrobe v55] Stat Spoofer init skipped:", errStat) end
end -- Stat Spoofer scope
print("[RH Wardrobe v55] 100% Royale High MultiColor formatting (16 RBXMs + all items), articulated heels & HipHeight persistence, full rig visibility active.")
